	private void AI_120_HallowBoss_DashTo(Vector2 targetPosition)
	{
		DirectionTo(targetPosition);
		targetPosition += new Vector2(0f, -300f);
		if (Distance(targetPosition) > 200f)
		{
			targetPosition -= DirectionTo(targetPosition) * 100f;
		}
		Vector2 vector = targetPosition - base.Center;
		float lerpValue = Utils.GetLerpValue(100f, 600f, vector.Length(), clamped: true);
		float num = vector.Length();
		if (num > 18f)
		{
			num = 18f;
		}
		velocity = Vector2.Lerp(vector.SafeNormalize(Vector2.Zero) * num, vector / 6f, lerpValue);
	}

	public bool Boss_CanShootExtraAt(int playerIndex, int rotationIndexToAttack, int rotationSize, float attackScanDistance, bool alwaysSkipMainTarget = true)
	{
		if (playerIndex == target && alwaysSkipMainTarget)
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (playerIndex % rotationSize != rotationIndexToAttack)
		{
			return false;
		}
		Player player = Main.player[playerIndex];
		if (!player.active || player.dead || !playerInteraction[playerIndex])
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (Distance(player.Center) > attackScanDistance)
		{
			return false;
		}
		return true;
	}

```
1	// Boss AI 1:1 移植(Terarria1456 NPC.cs SetDefaults 指定 aiStyle 的专用核)。
2	// 由 Enemy.fixedUpdate 的 aiStyle 分发调用;弹幕用 MagicProj/投射桶,部件用 fromVanilla。
3	// 各核保留原版状态机主链与特征机制(移速/计时/阶段),专家专属分支注释标明。
4	import type { Enemy } from './Enemy';
5	import type { GameHooks } from './types';
6	import type { Player } from './Player';
7	import { TILE } from '../core/constants';
8	import { MagicProj } from './MagicProj';
9	import { Arrow } from './Arrow';
10	
11	const addProj = (game: GameHooks, p: MagicProj) => {
12	  (game as unknown as { entities: { add: (e: unknown, b: string) => void } }).entities.add(p, 'projectiles');
13	};
14	export const addEnemy = (game: GameHooks, e: Enemy) => {
15	  const em = (game as unknown as { entities?: { nextId?: number; add?: (en: unknown, b: string) => void } }).entities;
16	  if (em?.nextId !== undefined) e.id = em.nextId++;
17	  em?.add?.(e, 'enemies');
18	  return e;
19	};
20	
21	/** AI_011 骷髅王战斗状态机(NPC.cs:21904-22288 完整核;守卫分支已在 skeletronHeadAI)。
22	 *  ai0=初始化(双手 36 生成);ai1: 0=悬浮(800t)→1=旋冲(400t)循环;2=守卫/白天;3=离场。
23	 *  悬浮: Y 拉向玩家上方 250(0.02/±2),X 朝玩家 0.05/±8;旋冲: rotation+=0.3*dir、
24	 *  朝玩家 1.5 倍速、伤害 ×1.3、防御 -10(:22026-22179)。 */
25	export function skeletronBossAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
26	  const isGuardian = e.vanillaId === 68;
27	  // 初始化:生成双手 36(:21914-21931)
28	  if (!e.bInit) {
29	    e.bInit = true;
30	    if (!isGuardian) {
31	      for (const side of [-1, 1]) {
32	        const h = spawnPart(game, e, 36);
33	        if (h) { h.ai0 = side; h.ai1 = e.id; h.ai3 = 150; h.bInit = true; }
34	      }
35	    }
36	  }
37	  // 白天 → ai1=2(守卫/离场语义);玩家死/超 2000px → 3
38	  if (game.world.clock.isDay && !isGuardian && e.ai1 !== 3) e.ai1 = 2;
39	  if (!player || Math.abs(e.cx - player.cx) > 2000 || Math.abs(e.cy - player.cy) > 2000) {
40	    if (!player || Math.abs(e.cx - player.cx) > 2000 || Math.abs(e.cy - player.cy) > 2000) e.ai1 = 3;
41	  }
42	  if (e.ai1 === 2) {
43	    // 守卫恒速直追(伤害 9999 语义)
44	    e.def.damage = isGuardian ? 9999 : e.def.damage;
45	    const dx = player!.cx - e.cx, dy = player!.cy - e.cy;
46	    const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
47	    e.vx = (dx / d) * 8; e.vy = (dy / d) * 8;
48	    e.x += e.vx; e.y += e.vy;
49	    return;
50	  }
51	  if (e.ai1 === 3) { e.y += 4; return; }
52	  if (!player) return;
53	
54	  e.ai2 += 1;
55	  if (e.ai1 === 0) {
56	    // 悬浮段(:22046-22091)
57	    if (e.ai2 >= 800) { e.ai2 = 0; e.ai1 = 1; }
58	    // 回悬浮:恢复冲撞前的伤害/防御基值
59	    const base = baseStats.get(e);
60	    if (base) { e.def.damage = base.dmg; e.def.defense = base.def; }
61	    if (e.y > player.y - 250) {
62	      if (e.vy > 0) e.vy *= 0.98;
63	      e.vy -= 0.02;
64	      if (e.vy > 2) e.vy = 2;
65	    } else {
66	      if (e.vy < 0) e.vy *= 0.98;
67	      e.vy += 0.02;
68	      if (e.vy < -2) e.vy = -2;
69	    }
70	    if (e.cx > player.cx) {
71	      if (e.vx > 0) e.vx *= 0.98;
72	      e.vx -= 0.05;
73	      if (e.vx > 8) e.vx = 8;
74	    } else {
75	      if (e.vx < 0) e.vx *= 0.98;
76	      e.vx += 0.05;
77	      if (e.vx < -8) e.vx = -8;
78	    }
79	  } else if (e.ai1 === 1) {
80	    // 旋冲段(:22115-22179):rot+=0.3*dir,1.5 倍速直冲,伤害 ×1.3,防御 -10。
81	    // 原版在进入冲撞时一次性应用——每 tick 重乘会把伤害放大 1.3^400,故缓存基值按基值设置
82	    if (e.ai2 >= 400) { e.ai2 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; }
83	    let base = baseStats.get(e);
84	    if (!base) { base = baseOf(e); }
85	    e.def.defense = Math.max(0, base.def - 10);
86	    e.def.damage = Math.round(base.dmg * 1.3);
87	    e.spin += (e.facing || 1) * 0.3;
88	    const dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - e.cy;
89	    const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
90	    const spd = 12; // (1.5x 追击速)
91	    e.vx += ((dx / d) * spd - e.vx) * 0.1;
92	    e.vy += ((dy / d) * spd - e.vy) * 0.1;
93	  }
94	  e.facing = e.vx > 0 ? 1 : -1;
95	  e.x += e.vx; e.y += e.vy;
96	}
97	
98	/** Boss 伤害/防御基值缓存(状态机每 tick 从基值派生临时修正,防重复乘膨胀) */
99	const baseStats = new WeakMap<Enemy, { dmg: number; def: number }>();
100	const baseOf = (e: Enemy) => {
101	  let b = baseStats.get(e);
102	  if (!b) { b = { dmg: e.def.damage, def: e.def.defense ?? 0 }; baseStats.set(e, b); }
103	  return b;
104	};
105	
106	let enemyCtor: (typeof import('./Enemy'))['Enemy'] | null = null;
107	/** Enemy 构造注入(Enemy.ts import 时回填,避免循环依赖) */
108	export function bindEnemyCtor(c: (typeof import('./Enemy'))['Enemy']) { enemyCtor = c; }
109	
110	export function spawnPart(game: GameHooks, src: Enemy, id: number): Enemy | null {
111	  if (!enemyCtor) return null;
112	  const p = enemyCtor.fromVanilla(id, src.cx, src.cy);
113	  if (!p) return null;
114	  return addEnemy(game, p);
115	}
116	
117	/** AI_012 骷髅王手(NPC.cs:22289-22400):锚定头(ai1=头 id),头离场 → 自毁;
118	 *  头非悬浮态 → 漂在头两侧(±120×ai0, 头上方 100);悬浮态 → 缓慢环绕(±200, +230)。
119	 *  每 300t(ai3 计)朝玩家挥击冲刺。 */
120	export function skeletronHandAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
121	  const headId = e.ai1;
122	  const enemies = game.enemies() as unknown[];
123	  const head = enemies.find((n) => (n as Enemy).id === headId) as Enemy | undefined;
124	  if (!head || head.vanilla?.aiStyle !== 11 || head.dead) { e.dead = true; return; }
125	  if (head.ai1 === 3) { e.dead = true; return; }
126	  e.facing = -(e.ai0 || 1);
127	  if (!player) { e.x += e.vx; e.y += e.vy; return; }
128	  // 缓慢环绕(头悬浮态);头冲撞态则回到侧位
129	  const ty = head.y - 100;
130	  const tx = head.x + head.w / 2 - e.w / 2 - 120 * (e.ai0 || 1);
131	  if (head.ai1 === 0) {
132	    e.ai3 += 1;
133	    if (e.ai3 >= 300) { e.ai3 = -120; } // 负值=挥击中
134	    if (e.ai3 < 0) {
135	      // 挥击:朝玩家冲刺 8 速
136	      const dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - e.cy;
137	      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
138	      e.vx += ((dx / d) * 8 - e.vx) * 0.15;
139	      e.vy += ((dy / d) * 8 - e.vy) * 0.15;
140	    } else {
141	      // 环绕位:头右/左 200px、下方 230(原版 num200 系)
142	      const ox = head.x + head.w / 2 - e.w / 2 - 200 * (e.ai0 || 1);
143	      const oy = head.y + 230;
144	      e.vx += ((ox - e.x) * 0.02 - e.vx) * 0.1;
145	      e.vy += ((oy - e.y) * 0.02 - e.vy) * 0.1;
146	    }
147	  } else {
148	    // 头冲撞中:快速回侧位
149	    e.vx += ((tx - e.x) * 0.05 - e.vx) * 0.2;
150	    e.vy += ((ty - e.y) * 0.05 - e.vy) * 0.2;
151	  }
152	  e.x += e.vx; e.y += e.vy;
153	}
154	
155	/** AI_015 史莱姆王(NPC.cs SetDefaults case 50 / AI_015):大跳朝玩家 + 受击攒怒 +
156	 *  落地砸出小史莱姆(30% 概率)。经典模式无传送(专家专属),暂不移植传送。 */
157	export function kingSlimeAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
158	  if (!player) return;
159	  // Enemy.ai0 默认 -1120(史莱姆族约定);原版 AI_015 的 ai[0] 从 0 起步 → 首帧复位
160	  if (!e.bInit) { e.bInit = true; e.ai0 = 0; }
161	  e.ai0 += 1;
162	  if (!e.onGround) {
163	    e.vy += 0.35; // 重力(原版大跳重力)
164	    // 空中水平减速朝玩家
165	    if (e.vx > 0) e.vx -= 0.02; else if (e.vx < 0) e.vx += 0.02;
166	  } else {
167	    // 落地:砸压判定 + 30% 出小史莱姆(原版 SpawnNPC(16) 语义)
168	    if (e.ai0 > 10) {
169	      const dx = player.cx - e.cx;
170	      e.vy = -8 - Math.random() * 3; // 大跳(原版 -8~-11)
171	      e.vx = Math.max(-7, Math.min(7, dx * 0.06));
172	      if (Math.random() < 0.3) {
173	        for (let k = 0; k < 2; k++) {
174	          const s = spawnPart(game, e, 16);
175	          if (s) { s.x += (Math.random() - 0.5) * 60; s.vy = -4; }
176	        }
177	      }
178	      game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.y + e.h, '#3080FF', 8, 2.2);
179	    }
180	  }
181	  e.x += e.vx;
182	  e.y += e.vy;
183	  // 地形碰撞(实心块)
184	  collideGround(e, game);
185	}
186	
187	/** AI_054 克苏鲁之脑(NPC.cs AI_054 + 267 爬行者环):
188	 *  一阶段(爬行者存活时无敌:dontTakeDamage)脑体悬空不动,爬行者环绕玩家;
189	 *  爬行者全灭 → 二阶段:瞬移冲刺(3 点环绕) + 幻影(简化:冲刺时半透明)。 */
190	export function brainOfCthulhuAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
191	  if (!player) return;
192	  const enemies = game.enemies() as unknown[];
193	  // 初次:补生成爬行者(原版 371-393 只)——必须先于爬行者计数,
194	  // 否则首 tick 爬行者为 0 直接落二阶段,永远不生成
195	  if (!e.bInit) {
196	    e.bInit = true;
197	    for (let k = 0; k < 8; k++) {
198	      const c = spawnPart(game, e, 267);
199	      if (c) {
200	        const ang = (k / 8) * Math.PI * 2;
201	        c.x = player.cx + Math.cos(ang) * 150;
202	        c.y = player.cy + Math.sin(ang) * 150;
203	      }
204	    }
205	  }
206	  const creepers = enemies.filter((n) => (n as Enemy).vanillaId === 267 && !(n as Enemy).dead);
207	  if (creepers.length > 0) {
208	    // 一阶段:无敌 + 悬浮微移(原版 ai[0]=0 悬空不动;爬行者环攻击)
209	    e.iframes = 2;
210	    e.vx *= 0.9; e.vy *= 0.9;
211	    e.x += e.vx; e.y += e.vy;
212	    return;
213	  }
214	  // 二阶段:瞬移冲刺循环(ai0 计 60t 一冲)
215	  e.ai0 += 1;
216	  e.spawnAlpha = 0;
217	  if (e.ai0 % 60 === 0) {
218	    // 瞬移到玩家周围随机点,朝玩家冲刺
219	    const ang = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
220	    e.x = player.cx + Math.cos(ang) * 200 - e.w / 2;
221	    e.y = player.cy + Math.sin(ang) * 200 - e.h / 2;
222	    const dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - e.cy;
223	    const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
224	    e.vx = (dx / d) * 9; e.vy = (dy / d) * 9;
225	    game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.cy, '#A03050', 10, 2);
226	  }
227	  e.x += e.vx; e.y += e.vy;
228	}
229	
230	/** 267 爬行者(NPC.cs AI_055 简化):环绕主人/玩家漂移 + 接触伤害 */
231	export function creeperAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
232	  if (!player) { e.x += e.vx; e.y += e.vy; return; }
233	  e.ai0 += 0.02;
234	  // 环绕玩家(半径 100-160 摆动)
235	  const r = 130 + Math.sin(e.aiT * 0.03) * 30;
236	  const tx = player.cx - e.w / 2 + Math.cos(e.ai0) * r;
237	  const ty = player.cy - e.h / 2 + Math.sin(e.ai0) * r;
238	  e.vx += (tx - e.x) * 0.01;
239	  e.vy += (ty - e.y) * 0.01;
240	  e.vx *= 0.92; e.vy *= 0.92;
241	  e.x += e.vx; e.y += e.vy;
242	  void game;
243	}
244	
245	// ================= 机械三王(NPC.cs AI_030-037,1.4.5.6 行号见各核注释) =================
246	// 经典模式参数 1:1;专家/大师/FTW 分支仅注释保留。
247	
248	/** 原版速度逼近(L26539-26670 雏形):朝目标速度单轴步进,符号翻转时双倍步进 */
249	export function accelTo(v: number, target: number, a: number): number {
250	  if (v < target) { v += a; if (v < 0 && target > 0) v += a; }
251	  else if (v > target) { v -= a; if (v > 0 && target < 0) v -= a; }
252	  return v;
253	}
254	/** 朝 (tx,ty) 以速度 spd/加速度 acc 追赶(vanilla numXXX = spd/dist 归一后 accelTo) */
255	export function seek(e: Enemy, tx: number, ty: number, spd: number, acc: number): void {
256	  const dx = tx - e.cx, dy = ty - e.cy;
257	  const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
258	  e.vx = accelTo(e.vx, (dx / d) * spd, acc);
259	  e.vy = accelTo(e.vy, (dy / d) * spd, acc);
260	}
261	/** 直线视线(原版 Collision.CanHit 近似:8px 采样无实心块) */
262	export function losClear(game: GameHooks, x0: number, y0: number, x1: number, y1: number): boolean {
263	  const st = game.world.store;
264	  const steps = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(Math.hypot(x1 - x0, y1 - y0) / 8));
265	  for (let i = 1; i < steps; i++) {
266	    const t = i / steps;
267	    const tx = Math.floor((x0 + (x1 - x0) * t) / TILE), ty = Math.floor((y0 + (y1 - y0) * t) / TILE);
268	    if (st.inBounds(tx, ty) && st.isSolid(tx, ty)) return false;
269	  }
270	  return true;
271	}
272	/** Boss 弹幕:直飞(grav 0)+ 原版 Projectile_N 贴图 */
273	export function shoot(game: GameHooks, x: number, y: number, vx: number, vy: number, dmg: number, projId: number, life = 300): void {
274	  (game as unknown as { entities: { add: (e: unknown, b: string) => void } }).entities.add(
275	    new Arrow(x, y, vx, vy, dmg, 0, projId, null, { grav: 0, life, pierce: 1 }), 'projectiles');
276	}
277	
278	/** AI_030/031 双子(NPC.cs:26497-27735):雷神之眼 125 / 魔神之眼 126。
279	 *  旋转持续瞄准玩家(0.1/0.15 步进+绕圈处理);白天/玩家死 → 上飞离场;
280	 *  一阶段(血>40%):侧向悬浮(300/400)→4-10 连冲(12/13 速);二阶段变身自旋;
281	 *  三阶段:雷眼 激光 83→100(伤害×1.5 防+10),魔眼 火焰喷射 101(伤害×1.5 防+18)。 */
282	export function twinsAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null, spaz: boolean) {
283	  if (!e.bInit) { e.bInit = true; e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; e.ai3 = 0; }
284	  if (!player) { e.x += e.vx; e.y += e.vy; return; }
285	  // ---- 旋转瞄准(num427/num471:atan2(dy-59, 自-玩)+π/2,步进 0.1/0.15) ----
286	  let target = Math.atan2(e.y + e.h - 59 - (player.y + player.h / 2), e.cx - player.cx) + Math.PI / 2;
287	  if (target < 0) target += 6.283; else if (target > 6.283) target -= 6.283;
288	  const step = spaz ? 0.15 : 0.1;
289	  let rot = e.spin;
290	  if (rot < target) { rot += (target - rot > Math.PI ? -step : step); }
291	  else if (rot > target) { rot += (rot - target > Math.PI ? step : -step); }
292	  if (Math.abs(rot - target) < step) rot = target;
293	  if (rot < 0) rot += 6.283; else if (rot > 6.283) rot -= 6.283;
294	  e.spin = rot;
295	
296	  // 白天/玩家死:上飞离场(Game.ts 日出剔除发"逃走"提示)
297	  if (game.world.clock.isDay || player.dead) {
298	    e.vy -= 0.04;
299	    e.x += e.vx; e.y += e.vy;
300	    return;
301	  }
302	
303	  // ---- 一阶段(ai0=0) ----
304	  if (e.ai0 === 0) {
305	    if (e.ai1 === 0) {
306	      // 侧向悬浮:雷眼 玩家侧 300/上方 300 速 7/0.1;魔眼 侧 400 速 12/0.4
307	      const spd = spaz ? 12 : 7, acc = spaz ? 0.4 : 0.1;
308	      const side = e.cx < player.x + player.w ? -1 : 1;
309	      const offX = spaz ? 400 : 300, offY = spaz ? 0 : 300;
310	      const tx = player.cx + side * offX, ty = player.cy - offY;
311	      const hoverDist = Math.hypot(tx - e.cx, ty - e.cy);
312	      seek(e, tx, ty, spd, acc);
313	      e.ai2 += 1;
314	      if (e.ai2 >= 600) { e.ai1 = 1; e.ai2 = 0; e.ai3 = 0; }
315	      // 悬浮期弹幕:魔眼 每 60t 诅咒焰 96(25 伤 12 速);雷眼 位于玩家上方且距悬浮点<400 时绿激光 83(20 伤 9 速)
316	      if (spaz) {
317	        e.ai3 += 1;
318	        if (e.ai3 >= 60) {
319	          e.ai3 = 0;
320	          const dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - e.cy;
321	          const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
322	          shoot(game, e.cx + dx / d * 4, e.cy + dy / d * 4,
323	            dx / d * 12 + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 4, dy / d * 12 + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 4, 25, 96);
324	        }
325	      } else if (e.y + e.h < player.y && hoverDist < 400) {
326	        e.ai3 += 1;
327	        if (e.ai3 >= 60) {
328	          e.ai3 = 0;
329	          const dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - e.cy;
330	          const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
331	          shoot(game, e.cx + dx / d * 15, e.cy + dy / d * 15,
332	            dx / d * 9 + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 6.4, dy / d * 9 + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 6.4, 20, 83);
333	        }
334	      }
335	    } else if (e.ai1 === 1) {
336	      // 冲撞:一次性朝玩家定速(雷眼 12/魔眼 13)
337	      e.spin = target;
338	      const dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - e.cy;
339	      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
340	      const spd = spaz ? 13 : 12;
341	      e.vx = dx / d * spd; e.vy = dy / d * spd;
342	      e.ai1 = 2;
343	    } else if (e.ai1 === 2) {
344	      e.ai2 += 1;
345	      if (e.ai2 >= (spaz ? 8 : 25)) {
346	        e.vx *= 0.96; e.vy *= 0.96;
347	        if (Math.abs(e.vx) < 0.1) e.vx = 0;
348	        if (Math.abs(e.vy) < 0.1) e.vy = 0;
349	      } else {
350	        e.spin = Math.atan2(e.vy, e.vx) - Math.PI / 2;
351	      }
352	      if (e.ai2 >= (spaz ? 42 : 70)) {
353	        e.ai3 += 1; e.ai2 = 0;
354	        e.spin = target;
355	        if (e.ai3 >= (spaz ? 10 : 4)) { e.ai1 = 0; e.ai3 = 0; }
356	        else e.ai1 = 1;
357	      }
358	    }
359	    if (e.hp < e.maxHp * 0.4) { e.ai0 = 1; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; e.ai3 = 0; }
360	    e.x += e.vx; e.y += e.vy;
361	    return;
362	  }
363	
364	  // ---- 变身(ai0=1 自旋加速 / ai0=2 减速,各 100t;ai2=角速度 0↔0.5) ----
365	  if (e.ai0 === 1 || e.ai0 === 2) {
366	    if (e.ai0 === 1) { e.ai2 += 0.005; if (e.ai2 > 0.5) e.ai2 = 0.5; }
367	    else { e.ai2 -= 0.005; if (e.ai2 < 0) e.ai2 = 0; }
368	    e.spin += e.ai2;
369	    e.ai1 += 1;
370	    if (e.ai1 >= 100) {
371	      e.ai0 += 1; e.ai1 = 0;
372	      if (e.ai0 === 3) e.ai2 = 0;
373	      else {
374	        game.playSfx('roar', 0.7);
375	        game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.cy, '#FF6040', 20, 2);
376	      }
377	    }
378	    e.vx *= 0.98; e.vy *= 0.98;
379	    if (Math.abs(e.vx) < 0.1) e.vx = 0;
380	    if (Math.abs(e.vy) < 0.1) e.vy = 0;
381	    e.x += e.vx; e.y += e.vy;
382	    return;
383	  }
384	
385	  // ---- 三阶段(ai0=3):伤害×1.5,防御 +(雷眼10/魔眼18) ----
386	  const b = baseOf(e);
387	  e.def.damage = Math.round(b.dmg * 1.5);
388	  e.def.defense = b.def + (spaz ? 18 : 10);
389	  if (e.ai1 === 0) {
390	    // 上方悬浮:雷眼 玩家正上 300(8/0.15,300t→冲);魔眼 侧 180 同高(4/0.1,400t→冲)+ 火焰喷射 101
391	    const spd = spaz ? 4 : 8, acc = spaz ? 0.1 : 0.15;
392	    const offX = spaz ? 180 : 0, offY = spaz ? 0 : 300;
393	    const side = e.cx < player.x + player.w ? -1 : 1;
394	    seek(e, player.cx + side * offX, player.cy - offY, spd, acc);
395	    e.ai2 += 1;
396	    if (e.ai2 >= (spaz ? 400 : 300)) { e.ai1 = 1; e.ai2 = 0; e.ai3 = 0; }
397	    e.spin = Math.atan2(player.cy - e.cy, player.cx - e.cx) - Math.PI / 2;
398	    if (e.aiT % (spaz ? 8 : 180) === 0 && losClear(game, e.cx, e.cy, player.cx, player.cy)) {
399	      const dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - e.cy;
400	      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
401	      const spdP = spaz ? 6 : 8.5;
402	      const dmgP = spaz ? 30 : 25;
403	      const pid = spaz ? 101 : 100;
404	      const lead = spaz ? 4 : 15;
405	      shoot(game, e.cx + dx / d * lead, e.cy + dy / d * lead,
406	        dx / d * spdP + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.8, dy / d * spdP + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.8, dmgP, pid);
407	    }
408	  } else if (e.ai1 === 1) {
409	    // 冲撞:雷眼 侧 340 8/0.2 / 180t 循环;魔眼 直冲 14 速(50/80t 减速,连 6)
410	    if (spaz) {
411	      e.spin = target;
412	      const dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - e.cy;
413	      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
414	      e.vx = dx / d * 14; e.vy = dy / d * 14;
415	      e.ai1 = 2;
416	    } else {
417	      const side = e.cx < player.x + player.w ? -1 : 1;
418	      seek(e, player.cx + side * 340, player.cy, 8, 0.2);
419	      e.spin = Math.atan2(player.cy - e.cy, player.cx - e.cx) - Math.PI / 2;
420	      if (e.aiT % 60 === 0 && losClear(game, e.cx, e.cy, player.cx, player.cy)) {
421	        const dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - e.cy;
422	        const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
423	        shoot(game, e.cx + dx / d * 15, e.cy + dy / d * 15, dx / d * 9, dy / d * 9, 18, 100);
424	      }
425	      e.ai2 += 1;
426	      if (e.ai2 >= 180) { e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; e.ai3 = 0; }
427	    }
428	  } else if (e.ai1 === 2 && spaz) {
429	    // 魔眼冲撞减速段(50t 起阻尼,80t 一循环,6 循环回悬浮)
430	    e.ai2 += 1;
431	    if (e.ai2 >= 50) {
432	      e.vx *= 0.93; e.vy *= 0.93;
433	      if (Math.abs(e.vx) < 0.1) e.vx = 0;
434	      if (Math.abs(e.vy) < 0.1) e.vy = 0;
435	    } else {
436	      e.spin = Math.atan2(e.vy, e.vx) - Math.PI / 2;
437	    }
438	    if (e.ai2 >= 80) {
439	      e.ai3 += 1; e.ai2 = 0;
440	      e.spin = target;
441	      if (e.ai3 >= 6) { e.ai1 = 0; e.ai3 = 0; } else e.ai1 = 1;
442	    }
443	  } else if (e.ai1 === 2) {
444	    // 雷眼冲撞减速段(25t 起阻尼,70t 一循环,4 循环回悬浮)
445	    e.ai2 += 1;
446	    if (e.ai2 >= 25) {
447	      e.vx *= 0.96; e.vy *= 0.96;
448	      if (Math.abs(e.vx) < 0.1) e.vx = 0;
449	      if (Math.abs(e.vy) < 0.1) e.vy = 0;
450	    } else {
451	      e.spin = Math.atan2(e.vy, e.vx) - Math.PI / 2;
452	    }
453	    if (e.ai2 >= 70) {
454	      e.ai3 += 1; e.ai2 = 0;
455	      e.spin = target;
456	      if (e.ai3 >= 4) { e.ai1 = 0; e.ai3 = 0; } else e.ai1 = 1;
457	    }
458	  }
459	  e.x += e.vx; e.y += e.vy;
460	}
461	
462	/** AI_032 机械骷髅王头 127(NPC.cs:27736-28054):初生成 4 部件;
463	 *  ai1: 0=悬浮(Y 带 200-500/X 窗 ±100,600t)→1=旋冲(防伤×2,2 速,400t);
464	 *  2=白天狂暴(伤/防 9999,10+距离/100 速追杀);3=玩家死/远 6000px 离场。 */
465	export function skeletronPrimeAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
466	  // 初始化:生成 128/129/130/131(ai0=侧 ±1, ai1=头 id;130/131 ai3=150 预热)
467	  if (!e.bInit) {
468	    e.bInit = true;
469	    const specs: Array<[number, number]> = [[128, -1], [129, 1], [130, -1], [131, 1]];
470	    for (const [id, side] of specs) {
471	      const p = spawnPart(game, e, id);
472	      if (p) { p.ai0 = side; p.ai1 = e.id; p.ai3 = id === 130 || id === 131 ? 150 : 0; p.bInit = true; }
473	    }
474	  }
475	  // 每 tick 从基值重置(vanilla damage=defDamage/defense=defDefense 置顶)
476	  const b = baseOf(e);
477	  e.def.damage = b.dmg;
478	  e.def.defense = b.def;
479	  // 玩家死亡(=无有效目标):按离场处理(原版 TargetClosest 落空 → ai[1]=3)
480	  if (!player) e.ai1 = 3;
481	  // 玩家死/超 6000px → 离场
482	  if (!player || Math.abs(e.cx - player.cx) > 6000 || Math.abs(e.cy - player.cy) > 6000) e.ai1 = 3;
483	  if (!player) { e.x += e.vx; e.y += e.vy; return; }
484	  // 白天 → 狂暴(原版不逃走,持续 9999 追杀;Game.ts 日出剔除对 127 豁免)
485	  if (game.world.clock.isDay && e.ai1 !== 3 && e.ai1 !== 2) {
486	    e.ai1 = 2;
487	    game.playSfx('roar', 0.8);
488	  }
489	  if (e.ai1 === 0) {
490	    // 悬浮段(:27787-27874):Y 带 [玩家-200, 玩家-500] 0.1/±2;X 窗 ±100 0.1/±8
491	    e.ai2 += 1;
492	    if (e.ai2 >= 600) { e.ai2 = 0; e.ai1 = 1; }
493	    e.spin = e.vx / 15;
494	    if (e.y > player.y - 200) {
495	      if (e.vy > 0) e.vy *= 0.98;
496	      e.vy -= 0.1;
497	      if (e.vy > 2) e.vy = 2;
498	    } else if (e.y < player.y - 500) {
499	      if (e.vy < 0) e.vy *= 0.98;
500	      e.vy += 0.1;
501	      if (e.vy < -2) e.vy = -2;
502	    }
503	    if (e.cx > player.cx + 100) {
504	      if (e.vx > 0) e.vx *= 0.98;
505	      e.vx -= 0.1;
506	      if (e.vx > 8) e.vx = 8;
507	    }
508	    if (e.cx < player.cx - 100) {
509	      if (e.vx < 0) e.vx *= 0.98;
510	      e.vx += 0.1;
511	      if (e.vx < -8) e.vx = -8;
512	    }
513	  } else if (e.ai1 === 1) {
514	    // 旋冲段(:27875-27970):防伤×2,spin+=0.3*dir,经典 2 速直冲,400t 回悬浮
515	    e.def.defense = b.def * 2;
516	    e.def.damage = b.dmg * 2;
517	    e.ai2 += 1;
518	    if (e.ai2 === 2) game.playSfx('roar', 0.6);
519	    if (e.ai2 >= 400) { e.ai2 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; }
520	    e.spin += (e.facing || 1) * 0.3;
521	    const dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - e.cy;
522	    const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
523	    e.vx = dx / d * 2; e.vy = dy / d * 2;
524	  } else if (e.ai1 === 2) {
525	    // 白天狂暴(:27971-28004):伤/防 9999,速 10+dist/100 钳 [8,32]
526	    e.def.damage = 9999;
527	    e.def.defense = 9999;
528	    e.spin += (e.facing || 1) * 0.3;
529	    const dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - e.cy;
530	    const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
531	    let spd = 10 + d / 100;
532	    spd = Math.max(8, Math.min(32, spd));
533	    e.vx = dx / d * spd; e.vy = dy / d * spd;
534	  } else {
535	    // 离场(:28043-28052):下坠离场
536	    e.vy += 0.1;
537	    if (e.vy < 0) e.vy *= 0.95;
538	    e.vx *= 0.95;
539	    if (e.vy > 13) e.vy = 13;
540	  }
541	  e.facing = e.vx > 0 ? 1 : -1;
542	  e.x += e.vx;
543	  e.y += e.vy;
544	}
545	
546	/** AI_033-036 Prime 四部件(NPC.cs:28055-29110):ai1=头 id,ai0=侧 ±1。
547	 *  33=锯 129(上冲俯冲 22 速)/34=激光 130(直冲 20 速,ai3 初始 150 预热)/
548	 *  35=钳 128(悬头侧,激光 102 0 伤)/36=炮 131(玩家上空悬停,激光 100 25 伤)。
549	 *  公共:头失效→自毁;头离场(ai1==3)→自毁;远离锚点 800px→追赶态 99。 */
550	export function primePartAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
551	  const headId = e.ai1;
552	  const head = (game.enemies() as Enemy[]).find((n) => n.id === headId && n.vanilla?.aiStyle === 32);
553	  // 头死亡/失效 → 自毁(原版 ai2+=10 到 50 清除;简化即时)
554	  if (!head || head.dead) { e.dead = true; return; }
555	  const side = e.ai0 || 1;
556	  e.facing = -side;
557	  const ax = head.cx - 200 * side, ay = head.y + 230; // 部件锚点(vanilla num528 系)
558	  const distA = Math.hypot(ax - e.cx, ay - e.cy);
559	  if (e.ai2 !== 99) { if (distA > 800) e.ai2 = 99; }
560	  else if (distA < 400) e.ai2 = 0;
561	  if (head.ai1 === 3) { e.dead = true; return; } // 头离场:部件跟随消散
562	  if (!player) { e.x += e.vx; e.y += e.vy; return; } // 玩家死亡:悬停不动(原版坠落的简化)
563	
564	  const style = e.vanilla?.aiStyle ?? 33;
565	  const hoverBand = (yLow: number, yHigh: number, acc: number, max: number) => {
566	    // Y 带 [head.y+yLow, head.y+yHigh] 内归位(vanilla 双侧 if)
567	    if (e.y > head.y + yLow) {
568	      if (e.vy > 0) e.vy *= 0.96;
569	      e.vy -= acc;
570	      if (e.vy > max) e.vy = max;
571	    } else if (e.y < head.y + yHigh) {
572	      if (e.vy < 0) e.vy *= 0.96;
573	      e.vy += acc;
574	      if (e.vy < -max) e.vy = -max;
575	    }
576	  };
577	  const xWindow = (winHigh: number, winLow: number, acc: number, max: number) => {
578	    // X 窗 [head.cx+winLow, head.cx+winHigh] 内归位(头侧偏移由调用方传正值)
579	    if (e.cx > head.cx + winHigh) {
580	      if (e.vx > 0) e.vx *= 0.96;
581	      e.vx -= acc;
582	      if (e.vx > max) e.vx = max;
583	    } else if (e.cx < head.cx + winLow) {
584	      if (e.vx < 0) e.vx *= 0.96;
585	      e.vx += acc;
586	      if (e.vx < -max) e.vx = -max;
587	    }
588	  };
589	
590	  if (e.ai2 === 99) {
591	    // 追赶态(:28083-28132):直奔头(0.5/±12 X,0.1/±8 Y)
592	    if (e.y > head.y) { if (e.vy > 0) e.vy *= 0.96; e.vy -= 0.1; if (e.vy > 8) e.vy = 8; }
593	    else if (e.y < head.y) { if (e.vy < 0) e.vy *= 0.96; e.vy += 0.1; if (e.vy < -8) e.vy = -8; }
594	    if (e.cx > head.cx) { if (e.vx > 0) e.vx *= 0.96; e.vx -= 0.5; if (e.vx > 12) e.vx = 12; }
595	    if (e.cx < head.cx) { if (e.vx < 0) e.vx *= 0.96; e.vx += 0.5; if (e.vx < -12) e.vx = -12; }
596	    e.x += e.vx; e.y += e.vy;
597	    return;
598	  }
599	
600	  if (style === 33) {
601	    // ---- 锯(NPC 129,:28134-28358) ----
602	    if (e.ai2 === 0 || e.ai2 === 3) {
603	      if (head.ai1 !== 0) {
604	        // 头冲撞中:7 速逼近玩家(0.05 步进/0.97 阻尼)
605	        seekSteer(e, player, 7, 0.05);
606	        e.ai3 += 1;
607	        if (e.ai3 >= 600) { e.ai2 = 0; e.ai3 = 0; }
608	      } else {
609	        e.ai3 += 1;
610	        if (e.ai3 >= 300) { e.ai2 = 1; e.ai3 = 0; }
611	        hoverBand(320, 260, 0.04, 3);
612	        if (e.cx > head.cx) { if (e.vx > 0) e.vx *= 0.96; e.vx -= 0.3; if (e.vx > 12) e.vx = 12; }
613	        if (e.cx < head.cx - 250) { if (e.vx < 0) e.vx *= 0.96; e.vx += 0.3; if (e.vx < -12) e.vx = -12; }
614	      }
615	      e.spin = Math.atan2(ay - e.cy, ax - e.cx) + Math.PI / 2;
616	    } else if (e.ai2 === 1) {
617	      // 升空俯冲:升至头上 200 → 22 速冲玩家
618	      e.spin = Math.atan2(ay - e.cy, ax - e.cx) + Math.PI / 2;
619	      e.vx *= 0.95;
620	      e.vy -= 0.1;
621	      if (e.vy < -8) e.vy = -8;
622	      if (e.y < head.y - 200 && player) {
623	        e.ai2 = 2;
624	        const dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - e.cy;
625	        const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
626	        e.vx = dx / d * 22; e.vy = dy / d * 22;
627	      }
628	    } else if (e.ai2 === 2) {
629	      // 俯冲到玩家下方/开始上移 → 回环绕(3)
630	      if (!player || e.y > player.y || e.vy < 0) e.ai2 = 3;
631	    } else if (e.ai2 === 5 && player) {
632	      if ((e.vx > 0 && e.cx > player.cx) || (e.vx < 0 && e.cx < player.cx)) e.ai2 = 0;
633	    }
634	  } else if (style === 34) {
635	    // ---- 激光(NPC 130,:28439-28638) ----
636	    if (e.ai2 === 0 || e.ai2 === 3) {
637	      if (head.ai1 !== 0) {
638	        // 头冲撞中:12 速突进(慢于 2 直接定速,否则阻尼)
639	        if (player) {
640	          const dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - e.cy;
641	          const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
642	          if (Math.abs(e.vx) + Math.abs(e.vy) < 2) { e.vx = dx / d * 12; e.vy = dy / d * 12; }
643	          else { e.vx *= 0.97; e.vy *= 0.97; }
644	        }
645	        e.ai3 += 1;
646	        if (e.ai3 >= 600) { e.ai2 = 0; e.ai3 = 0; }
647	      } else {
648	        e.ai3 += 1;
649	        if (e.ai3 >= 600) { e.ai2 = 1; e.ai3 = 0; }
650	        hoverBand(300, 230, 0.1, 3);
651	        if (e.cx > head.cx + 250) { if (e.vx > 0) e.vx *= 0.94; e.vx -= 0.3; if (e.vx > 9) e.vx = 9; }
652	        if (e.cx < head.cx) { if (e.vx < 0) e.vx *= 0.94; e.vx += 0.2; if (e.vx < -8) e.vx = -8; }
653	      }
654	      e.spin = Math.atan2(ay - e.cy, ax - e.cx) + Math.PI / 2;
655	    } else if (e.ai2 === 1) {
656	      // 升空直冲:升至头上 280 → 20 速冲玩家(初 ai3=150 → 首冲提前)
657	      const ax2 = head.cx - 280 * side, ay2 = head.y + 230;
658	      e.spin = Math.atan2(ay2 - e.cy, ax2 - e.cx) + Math.PI / 2;
659	      if (e.vy > 0) e.vy *= 0.9;
660	      e.vx = (e.vx * 5 + head.vx) / 6;
661	      e.vx += 0.5;
662	      e.vy -= 0.5;
663	      if (e.vy < -9) e.vy = -9;
664	      if (e.y < head.y - 280 && player) {
665	        e.ai2 = 2;
666	        const dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - e.cy;
667	        const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
668	        e.vx = dx / d * 20; e.vy = dy / d * 20;
669	      }
670	    } else if (e.ai2 === 2) {
671	      if (!player || e.y > player.y || e.vy < 0) {
672	        if (e.ai3 >= 4) { e.ai2 = 3; e.ai3 = 0; }
673	        else { e.ai2 = 1; e.ai3 += 1; }
674	      }
675	    } else if (e.ai2 === 4) {
676	      // 侧向横扫(原版与 5 互切;头冲撞分支偶尔进入)
677	      e.spin = Math.atan2(ay - e.cy, ax - e.cx) + Math.PI / 2;
678	      e.vy = (e.vy * 5 + head.vy) / 6;
679	      e.vx += 0.5;
680	      if (e.vx > 12) e.vx = 12;
681	      if (player && Math.abs(e.cx - head.cx) > 500) {
682	        e.ai2 = 5;
683	        const dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - e.cy;
684	        const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
685	        e.vx = dx / d * 17; e.vy = dy / d * 17;
686	      }
687	    } else if (e.ai2 === 5 && player && e.cx < player.cx - 100) {
688	      if (e.ai3 >= 4) { e.ai2 = 0; e.ai3 = 0; }
689	      else { e.ai2 = 4; e.ai3 += 1; }
690	    }
691	  } else if (style === 35) {
692	    // ---- 钳(NPC 128,:28653-28874) ----
693	    if (e.ai2 === 0) {
694	      if (head.ai1 !== 0) {
695	        hoverBand(-100, -100, 0.07, 6);
696	        xWindow(-120 * side, -120 * side, 0.1, 8);
697	      } else {
698	        e.ai3 += 1;
699	        if (e.ai3 >= 1100) { e.ai2 = 1; e.ai3 = 0; }
700	        hoverBand(-150, -150, 0.04, 3);
701	        xWindow(200, 160, 0.2, 8);
702	      }
703	      e.spin = Math.atan2(ay - e.cy, ax - e.cx) + Math.PI / 2;
704	      // 反向激光 102(原版伤害 0 = 纯视觉威慑)每 140t
705	      if (e.aiT % 140 === 0) {
706	        const dx = -(ax - e.cx), dy = -(ay - e.cy);
707	        const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
708	        shoot(game, e.cx + dx / d * 4, e.cy + dy / d * 4,
709	          dx / d * 12 + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.8, dy / d * 12 + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.8, 0, 102);
710	      }
711	    } else if (e.ai2 === 1 && player) {
712	      e.ai3 += 1;
713	      if (e.ai3 >= 300) { e.ai2 = 0; e.ai3 = 0; }
714	      // 悬停:头 X / 玩家上方 80(6 速,0.04 X/0.08 Y,0.9 阻尼)
715	      seekSteer2(e, head.cx, player.cy - 80, 6, 0.04, 0.08);
716	      e.spin = Math.atan2(player.cy - e.cy, player.cx - e.cx) - Math.PI / 2;
717	      if (e.aiT % 40 === 0) {
718	        const dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - e.cy;
719	        const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
720	        shoot(game, e.cx + dx / d * 4, e.cy + dy / d * 4,
721	          dx / d * 10 + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.8, dy / d * 10 + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.8, 0, 102);
722	      }
723	    }
724	  } else if (style === 36) {
725	    // ---- 炮(NPC 131,:28889-29109;初 ai3=150 计入 800 门) ----
726	    if (e.ai2 === 0 || e.ai2 === 3) {
727	      if (head.ai1 !== 0) {
728	        hoverBand(-100, -100, 0.07, 6);
729	        xWindow(-120 * side, -120 * side, 0.1, 8);
730	      } else {
731	        e.ai3 += 1;
732	        if (e.ai3 >= 800) { e.ai2 = 1; e.ai3 = 0; }
733	        hoverBand(-100, -100, 0.1, 3);
734	        xWindow(-180 * side, -180 * side, 0.14, 8);
735	      }
736	      if (player) {
737	        e.spin = Math.atan2(player.cy - e.cy, player.cx - e.cx) - Math.PI / 2;
738	        if (e.aiT % 200 === 0) {
739	          const dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - e.cy;
740	          const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
741	          shoot(game, e.cx + dx / d * 8, e.cy + dy / d * 8,
742	            dx / d * 8 + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 4, dy / d * 8 + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 4, 25, 100);
743	        }
744	      }
745	    } else if (e.ai2 === 1 && player) {
746	      e.ai3 += 1;
747	      if (e.ai3 >= 200) { e.ai2 = 0; e.ai3 = 0; }
748	      // 悬停玩家左侧 350/上方 20(7 速,0.1/0.03,0.9 阻尼)
749	      seekSteer2(e, player.cx - 350, player.cy - 20, 7, 0.1, 0.03);
750	      e.spin = Math.atan2(player.cy - e.cy, player.cx - e.cx) - Math.PI / 2;
751	      if (e.aiT % 80 === 0) {
752	        const dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - e.cy;
753	        const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
754	        shoot(game, e.cx + dx / d * 8, e.cy + dy / d * 8,
755	          dx / d * 10 + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 4, dy / d * 10 + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 4, 25, 100);
756	      }
757	    }
758	  }
759	  e.x += e.vx;
760	  e.y += e.vy;
761	}
762	
763	/** 原版"逼近目标速度"舵(:28161-28192 锯/激光共用):目标 = dir*spd,0.97 阻尼 ±0.05 步进 */
764	export function seekSteer(e: Enemy, target: { cx: number; cy: number }, spd: number, acc: number): void {
765	  const dx = target.cx - e.cx, dy = target.cy - e.cy;
766	  const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
767	  const tx = (dx / d) * spd, ty = (dy / d) * spd;
768	  if (e.vx > tx) { if (e.vx > 0) e.vx *= 0.97; e.vx -= acc; }
769	  if (e.vx < tx) { if (e.vx < 0) e.vx *= 0.97; e.vx += acc; }
770	  if (e.vy > ty) { if (e.vy > 0) e.vy *= 0.97; e.vy -= acc; }
771	  if (e.vy < ty) { if (e.vy < 0) e.vy *= 0.97; e.vy += acc; }
772	}
773	/** 同上但 X/Y 不同步进(钳/炮悬停态,:28817-28848/:29052-29083),0.9 阻尼 */
774	export function seekSteer2(e: Enemy, tx0: number, ty0: number, spd: number, accX: number, accY: number): void {
775	  const dx = tx0 - e.cx, dy = ty0 - e.cy;
776	  const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
777	  const tx = (dx / d) * spd, ty = (dy / d) * spd;
778	  if (e.vx > tx) { if (e.vx > 0) e.vx *= 0.9; e.vx -= accX; }
779	  if (e.vx < tx) { if (e.vx < 0) e.vx *= 0.9; e.vx += accX; }
780	  if (e.vy > ty) { if (e.vy > 0) e.vy *= 0.9; e.vy -= accY; }
781	  if (e.vy < ty) { if (e.vy < 0) e.vy *= 0.9; e.vy += accY; }
782	}
783	
784	/** AI_037 毁灭者 134-136(NPC.cs:50129-50624):头穿地飞行——
785	 *  空中:下坠 0.15/钳 16 并横漂向玩家;地下(实心/液体):全向舵向玩家(0.1/0.15 双档);
786	 *  白天/玩家死:加速下坠,过岩石层整链消散;体节 135 探针随机激光 100。
787	 *  段链 80 段(原版 GetDestroyerSegmentsCount;链距 44px)由头驱动收缩跟随。 */
788	export function destroyerAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
789	  if (!e.bInit) {
790	    e.bInit = true;
791	    // 原版 ai[0]==0 时头生成 80 段链(body 135×79 + tail 136;链距 44*scale)
792	    const segs = enemySpawnChain(e, 80);
793	    for (const s of segs) addEnemy(game, s);
794	  }
795	  const st = game.world.store;
796	  // 接地判定(flag2):AABB 外扩 1-2 格内实心块或液体>64(:50278-50298)
797	  let buried = false;
798	  const tx0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor(e.x / TILE) - 1), tx1 = Math.min(st.w - 1, Math.floor((e.x + e.w) / TILE) + 2);
799	  const ty0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor(e.y / TILE) - 1), ty1 = Math.min(st.h - 1, Math.floor((e.y + e.h) / TILE) + 2);
800	  for (let x = tx0; x <= tx1 && !buried; x++) {
801	    for (let y = ty0; y <= ty1; y++) {
802	      const i = st.idx(x, y);
803	      if ((st.flags[i] && st.isSolid(x, y)) || st.liquid[i] > 64) {
804	        if (e.x + e.w > x * TILE && e.x < x * TILE + 16 && e.y + e.h > y * TILE && e.y < y * TILE + 16) { buried = true; break; }
805	      }
806	    }
807	  }
808	
809	  // 白天/玩家死:下坠离场;过岩石层整链消散(:50337-50356)
810	  const dayFlee = game.world.clock.isDay || !player || player.dead;
811	  if (dayFlee) {
812	    e.vy += 1;
813	    if (e.y > game.world.groundLevel * TILE) e.vy += 1;
814	    e.spin = Math.atan2(e.vy, e.vx) + Math.PI / 2;
815	    e.x += e.vx; e.y += e.vy;
816	    driveChain(e, 44);
817	    if (e.y > game.world.rockLevel * TILE) {
818	      for (let s: Enemy | null = e; s; s = s.wormNext) s.dead = true;
819	    }
820	    return;
821	  }
822	
823	  const spd = 16, acc = 0.1, acc2 = 0.15;
824	  if (!buried) {
825	    // 空中(:50416-50458):下坠 0.15 钳 16;慢速时横漂,满速下坠时向玩家 X 修正
826	    e.vy += 0.15;
827	    if (e.vy > spd) e.vy = spd;
828	    const px = Math.floor(player.cx / TILE) * TILE, py = Math.floor(player.cy / TILE) * TILE;
829	    const sx = Math.floor(e.cx / TILE) * TILE, sy = Math.floor(e.cy / TILE) * TILE;
830	    const dx = px - sx, dy = py - sy;
831	    if (Math.abs(e.vx) + Math.abs(e.vy) < spd * 0.4) {
832	      e.vx += (e.vx < 0 ? -acc * 1.1 : acc * 1.1);
833	    } else if (e.vy === spd) {
834	      if (e.vx < dx) e.vx += acc;
835	      else if (e.vx > dx) e.vx -= acc;
836	    } else if (e.vy > 4) {
837	      e.vx += (e.vx < 0 ? acc * 0.9 : -acc * 0.9);
838	    }
839	    void dy;
840	  } else {
841	    // 地下(:50459-50585):目标速度 = 方向*16,分档加速(同向双轴 0.15→同向单轴 0.10+
842	    // 反轴轻推→主轴 0.11 次轴补速;含防卡死角交叉助推)
843	    const dx = Math.floor(player.cx / TILE) * TILE - Math.floor(e.cx / TILE) * TILE;
844	    const dy = Math.floor(player.cy / TILE) * TILE - Math.floor(e.cy / TILE) * TILE;
845	    const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
846	    const tvx = (dx / d) * spd, tvy = (dy / d) * spd;
847	    const sameX = (e.vx > 0 && tvx > 0) || (e.vx < 0 && tvx < 0);
848	    const sameY = (e.vy > 0 && tvy > 0) || (e.vy < 0 && tvy < 0);
849	    if (sameX && sameY) {
850	      if (e.vx < tvx) e.vx += acc2; else if (e.vx > tvx) e.vx -= acc2;
851	      if (e.vy < tvy) e.vy += acc2; else if (e.vy > tvy) e.vy -= acc2;
852	    }
853	    if (sameX || sameY) {
854	      if (e.vx < tvx) e.vx += acc; else if (e.vx > tvx) e.vx -= acc;
855	      if (e.vy < tvy) e.vy += acc; else if (e.vy > tvy) e.vy -= acc;
856	      if (Math.abs(tvy) < spd * 0.2 && ((e.vx > 0 && tvx < 0) || (e.vx < 0 && tvx > 0))) {
857	        e.vy += (e.vy > 0 ? acc * 2 : -acc * 2);
858	      }
859	      if (Math.abs(tvx) < spd * 0.2 && ((e.vy > 0 && tvy < 0) || (e.vy < 0 && tvy > 0))) {
860	        e.vx += (e.vx > 0 ? acc * 2 : -acc * 2);
861	      }
862	    } else if (Math.abs(dx) > Math.abs(dy)) {
863	      if (e.vx < tvx) e.vx += acc * 1.1; else if (e.vx > tvx) e.vx -= acc * 1.1;
864	      if (Math.abs(e.vx) + Math.abs(e.vy) < spd * 0.5) e.vy += (e.vy > 0 ? acc : -acc);
865	    } else {
866	      if (e.vy < tvy) e.vy += acc * 1.1; else if (e.vy > tvy) e.vy -= acc * 1.1;
867	      if (Math.abs(e.vx) + Math.abs(e.vy) < spd * 0.5) e.vx += (e.vx > 0 ? acc : -acc);
868	    }
869	  }
870	  e.spin = Math.atan2(e.vy, e.vx) + Math.PI / 2;
871	  e.x += e.vx;
872	  e.y += e.vy;
873	  // 段链跟随(linkDist 44 = 44*scale;原版 num24) + 探针弹幕
874	  driveChain(e, 44);
875	  if (!buried) {
876	    for (let s = e.wormNext; s; s = s.wormNext) {
877	      if (s.vanillaId !== 135 || s.dead) continue;
878	      // 原版 135:localAI[0] += Next(4),达 Next(1400,26000) 触发(期望 ~6850t/节);
879	      // 近似为每 tick 2/6850 概率 + 视线检查
880	      if (Math.random() < 2 / 6850 && losClear(game, s.cx, s.cy, player.cx, player.cy)) {
881	        const dx = player.cx - s.cx + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 40;
882	        const dy = player.cy - s.cy + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 40;
883	        const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
884	        shoot(game, s.cx + dx / d * 5, s.cy + dy / d * 5,
885	          dx / d * 8 + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 2, dy / d * 8 + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 2, 22, 100);
886	      }
887	    }
888	  }
889	}
890	
891	/** 生成蠕虫段链(Enemy.spawnWormChain 的外部包装;Destroyer 用 135/136) */
892	export function enemySpawnChain(head: Enemy, count: number): Enemy[] {
893	  const Ctor = enemyCtor;
894	  if (!Ctor) return [];
895	  return Ctor.spawnWormChain(head, count);
896	}
897	/** 头驱动段链收缩跟随(原版 :50374-50414,linkDist 可配) */
898	export function driveChain(head: Enemy, linkDist: number): void {
899	  for (let s = head.wormNext; s; s = s.wormNext) {
900	    const fx = s.wormFollow!;
901	    const dxC = fx.cx - s.cx;
902	    const dyC = fx.cy - s.cy;
903	    const dist = Math.hypot(dxC, dyC);
904	    if (dist > 0.01) {
905	      const shrink = (dist - linkDist) / dist;
906	      s.x += dxC * shrink;
907	      s.y += dyC * shrink;
908	      s.spin = Math.atan2(dyC, dxC) + Math.PI / 2;
909	      s.facing = dxC < 0 ? 1 : -1;
910	    }
911	  }
912	}
913	
914	// ---- 工具 ----
915	
916	function collideGround(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks) {
917	  const st = game.world.store;
918	  const tx = Math.floor(e.cx / TILE);
919	  const ty = Math.floor((e.y + e.h) / TILE);
920	  if (st.inBounds(tx, ty) && st.isSolid(tx, ty) && e.vy >= 0) {
921	    e.y = ty * TILE - e.h;
922	    e.vy = 0;
923	    e.onGround = true;
924	  } else {
925	    e.onGround = false;
926	  }
927	  const ty2 = Math.floor(e.y / TILE);
928	  if (st.inBounds(tx, ty2) && st.isSolid(tx, ty2) && e.vy < 0) {
929	    e.y = (ty2 + 1) * TILE;
930	    e.vy = 0;
931	  }
932	  const tx2 = Math.floor((e.x + e.w) / TILE);
933	  if (st.inBounds(tx2, Math.floor(e.cy / TILE)) && st.isSolid(tx2, Math.floor(e.cy / TILE)) && e.vx > 0) {
934	    e.x = tx2 * TILE - e.w; e.vx = 0;
935	  }
936	  const tx3 = Math.floor(e.x / TILE);
937	  if (st.inBounds(tx3, Math.floor(e.cy / TILE)) && st.isSolid(tx3, Math.floor(e.cy / TILE)) && e.vx < 0) {
938	    e.x = (tx3 + 1) * TILE; e.vx = 0;
939	  }
940	}
941

// 敌人实体：统一类 + 按 key 分支行为（史莱姆/僵尸/恶魔眼/蝙蝠/克苏鲁之眼及其仆从）
// + 原版 NPC 数据驱动路径（vanillaId）：属性/AI/音效/贴图来自 vanilla-npcs.json（SetDefaults 提取）
import { Entity } from './Entity';
import type { GameHooks } from './types';
import type { Player } from './Player';
import { ENEMY_DEFS, EnemyDef } from '../data/enemies';
import { vanillaNpc, vanillaSoundName, vanillaNpcDrops, type VanillaNpc } from '../data/vanillaNpcs';
import { GRAVITY, MAX_FALL_SPEED, TILE } from '../core/constants';
import { moveAndCollide } from '../physics/TileCollision';
import { Dart } from './Dart';
import { avoidWater } from './waterAvoid';
import { bindEnemyCtor, skeletronBossAI, skeletronHandAI, kingSlimeAI, brainOfCthulhuAI, creeperAI, twinsAI, skeletronPrimeAI, primePartAI, destroyerAI } from './bossAI';
import { RNG } from '../core/rng';

/** 原版 Boss 头/主体 id（部件不标记:击杀部件不应出 Boss 退场流程）。
 *  EoC4/世吞13-15(头13 为 Boss,身14尾15 不标)/骷髅王35+手36/地牢守卫68/史莱姆王50/
 *  血肉墙113/双子125,126/骷髅Prime127/毁灭者134/蜂后222/石巨人245/世纪之花262/克脑266/
 *  猪鲨370/月总核心398/异教徒439/光皇636/史莱姆皇后657 */
const VANILLA_BOSS_IDS = new Set([4, 13, 35, 50, 68, 113, 125, 126, 127, 134, 222, 245, 262, 266, 370, 398, 439, 636, 657]);

/** 原版路径 key（v_*）的占位 def，fromVanilla 会整体覆写 */
const PLACEHOLDER_DEF: EnemyDef = {
  key: 'v_placeholder', name: '?', hp: 1, damage: 0, knockbackResist: 0.5,
  width: 16, height: 16, mapColor: '#9A8FA0', gore: ['#9A8FA0', '#5E5566', '#C4BACC'],
  hitSound: ['NPC_Hit_1'], killedSound: ['NPC_Killed_1'], drops: [],
};

export class Enemy extends Entity {
  /** 原版 NPC id（数据驱动路径启用时非空） */
  vanillaId: number | null = null;
  vanilla: VanillaNpc | null = null;
  // ---- 蠕虫多段体（AI_006，NPC.cs:18046）：头 aiStyle 6，编号约定 头+1=身 头+2=尾 ----
  /** 链上紧随本段的一段（头 → 身×n → 尾） */
  wormNext: Enemy | null = null;
  /** 本段跟随的前一段（非空 = 本段是身体段，跳过 AI 只做跟随） */
  wormFollow: Enemy | null = null;
  /** 上一 tick 位置（段跟随用：段复制前一段的旧位置 = 经典贪吃蛇链） */
  prevX = 0; prevY = 0;

  /** AI_006 头部（L18645 通用常数 maxSpd=8 accel=0.07；穿墙直行；段链跟随） */
  private wormAI(game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
    const maxSpd = 8, accel = 0.07;
    // 朝向：有玩家朝玩家，无玩家缓慢巡游
    let dx: number, dy: number;
    if (player) { dx = player.cx - this.cx; dy = player.cy - this.cy; }
    else { dx = Math.cos(this.aiT * 0.02) * 10; dy = Math.sin(this.aiT * 0.013) * 10; }
    const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
    this.vx += (dx / d) * accel;
    this.vy += (dy / d) * accel;
    const spd = Math.hypot(this.vx, this.vy);
    if (spd > maxSpd) { this.vx = (this.vx / spd) * maxSpd; this.vy = (this.vy / spd) * maxSpd; }
    this.facing = this.vx > 0 ? 1 : -1;
    // 蠕虫穿墙：直接位移（原版 noTileCollide）
    this.x += this.vx;
    this.y += this.vy;
    // 段链跟随（原版 L52271-52308）：方向向量收缩维持 linkDist 间距——
    // shrink = (dist - linkDist)/dist；position += dxC*shrink（原版 num63/num64）
    for (let s = this.wormNext; s; s = s.wormNext) {
      const fx = s.wormFollow!;
      const dxC = fx.cx - s.cx;
      const dyC = fx.cy - s.cy;
      const dist = Math.hypot(dxC, dyC);
      if (dist > 0.01) {
        const linkDist = s.w;               // 原版 num64 = width
        const shrink = (dist - linkDist) / dist;
        s.x += dxC * shrink;
        s.y += dyC * shrink;
        s.facing = dxC < 0 ? 1 : -1;         // 原版 spriteDirection（L52305）
      }
    }
  }

  /** 由头生成段链（原版各 worm 的 NewNPC 链，NPC.cs:18174+）：body×n + tail */
  static spawnWormChain(head: Enemy, segCount: number): Enemy[] {
    const segs: Enemy[] = [];
    const bodyId = head.vanillaId! + 1, tailId = head.vanillaId! + 2;
    let prev = head;
    for (let k = 0; k < segCount; k++) {
      const id = k === segCount - 1 ? tailId : bodyId;
      const s = Enemy.fromVanilla(id, head.cx, head.cy);
      if (!s) continue;
      s.wormFollow = prev;
      prev.wormNext = s;
      prev = s;
      segs.push(s);
    }
    return segs;
  }


  /** 用原版数据造怪：属性/碰撞/音效全部来自 SetDefaults 提取值 */
  static fromVanilla(id: number, x: number, y: number): Enemy | null {
    const v = vanillaNpc(id);
    if (!v) return null;
    const e = new Enemy(`v_${id}`, x, y);
    e.vanillaId = id;
    e.vanilla = v;
    const hit = vanillaSoundName(v.HitSound) ?? 'NPC_Hit_1';
    const kill = vanillaSoundName(v.DeathSound) ?? 'NPC_Killed_1';
    const flying = v.noGravity || v.aiStyle === 2 || v.aiStyle === 5 || v.aiStyle === 14;
    e.def = {
      ...e.def,
      name: v.name, hp: v.lifeMax, damage: v.damage, defense: v.defense,
      // 原版 knockBackResist 是"承受击退的比例"（0.5=吃一半）；本仓库语义是
      // "抗性"（hurt(): resist<0.9 才生效，kbx*(1-resist)）→ 换算 1-比例
      knockbackResist: Math.max(0, Math.min(0.89, 1 - (v.knockBackResist ?? 0.5))),
      width: v.width, height: v.height, flying,
      boss: VANILLA_BOSS_IDS.has(id),
      nightOnly: v.aiStyle === 2 || v.aiStyle === 5, underground: false,
      mapColor: '#9A8FA0', gore: ['#9A8FA0', '#5E5566', '#C4BACC'],
      hitSound: [hit], killedSound: [kill], drops: v.critter ? [] : vanillaNpcDrops(id),
      // 小动物：无接触伤害、不夜行
      ...(v.critter ? { damage: 0, nightOnly: false } : {}),
    };
    e.hp = v.lifeMax;
    e.maxHp = v.lifeMax;
    e.w = v.width;
    e.h = v.height;
    e.spawnAlpha = v.alpha ?? 0; // 原版 SetDefaults alpha（静态不透明度，NPC.Opacity=1-alpha/255）
    // EoW 族 alpha=255 = 出生全透明渐显标记（其余 alpha 为静态不透明度,勿动）：

  fixedUpdate(dt: number, game: GameHooks) {
    this.prevX = this.x; this.prevY = this.y;
    this.aiT++;
    if (this.iframes > 0) this.iframes--;
    if (this.hpBarT > 0) this.hpBarT--;
    if (this.squash !== 0) this.squash *= 0.85;
    this.animT++;
    this.walkCycleT += Math.abs(this.vx); // 原版 zombie/fighter 行走帧按 |vx| 累加（L77072/L70216）

    // EoW 族出生渐显（每 tick alpha-1;原版 AI_006）——静态半透明不受影响
    if (this.alphaFade) {
      this.spawnAlpha = Math.max(0, this.spawnAlpha - 1);
      if (this.spawnAlpha === 0) this.alphaFade = false;
    }

    const player = (game as unknown as { player: Player }).player;
    const hasPlayer = !!player && !player.dead;

    // ---- 原版数据驱动路径：按 aiStyle 分发到已移植的 AI 家族（分发后落入共享尾段） ----
    // 蠕虫身体段（wormFollow 非空）无 AI：位置由头部 wormAI 沿链驱动，但仍走共享尾段（接触伤害等）
    // 小动物（critter）优先走被动游荡，绝不落入 default 的追击 fallback
    if (this.vanilla && !this.wormFollow && this.vanilla.critter) {
      this.critterWanderAI(game, hasPlayer ? player : null);
    } else if (this.vanilla && !this.wormFollow) {
      const p = hasPlayer ? player : null;
      switch (this.vanilla.aiStyle) {
        case 1: this.slimeAI(game, p); break;                          // AI_001 史莱姆
        case 2: this.floatEyeAI(game, p); break;                        // AI_002 飘浮眼（原版核）
        case 3: this.fighterAI(game, p); break;                        // AI_003 战士族（原版通用核）
        // EoC 传参例外:玩家死亡也要传原对象(cs:19931 dead 判定走逃离分支,
        // 冲刺计时合法冻结;传 null 会让 aiT 照走、状态机停摆 → 首冲时长翻倍)
        case 4: this.eocAI(game, (game as unknown as { player: Player }).player ?? null); break; // AI_004 克苏鲁之眼（NPC.cs:19909）
        case 5: this.swarmerAI(game, p); break;                         // AI_005 噬魂怪族（原版蜂群核）
        case 6: this.wormAI(game, p); break;                           // AI_006 蠕虫族（头）
        case 8: this.casterAI(game, p); break;                          // AI_008 法师族（传送+弹幕）
        case 10: this.cursedSkullAI(game, p); break;                     // AI_010 诅咒头骨（地牢飞行族）
        case 11:
          // 68 守卫走专用分支（bossAI.skeletronBossAI 注释"守卫分支已在 skeletronHeadAI"
          // 即此拆分：守卫恒速追击与骷髅王战斗状态机互不混用）
          if (this.vanillaId === 68) this.dungeonGuardianAI(game, p);
          else skeletronBossAI(this, game, p);
          break;                                                         // AI_011 骷髅王头战斗核/地牢守卫
        case 12: skeletronHandAI(this, game, p); break;                    // AI_012 骷髅王手(锚定头)
        case 20: this.blazingWheelAI(); break;                           // AI_020 火轮（地牢陷阱）
        case 21: this.spikeBallAI(game); break;                          // AI_021 刺球（地牢陷阱）
        case 14: this.batAI(game, p); break;                            // AI_014 蝙蝠/飞行族（1.4.5.6 原版核）
        case 16: this.swimAI(game, p); break;                           // AI_016 游泳族（鲨鱼/食人鱼）
        case 18: this.jellyfishAI(game, p); break;                      // AI_018 水母族（脉冲游动）
        case 22: this.ghostAI(game, p); break;                          // AI_022 幽灵族（穿墙缓速）
        case 13: this.plantAI(game, p); break;                          // AI_013 植物/攀爬者族（锚点伸展）
        case 15: kingSlimeAI(this, game, p); break;                       // AI_015 史莱姆王(大跳+小史莱姆)
        case 54: brainOfCthulhuAI(this, game, p); break;                  // AI_054 克脑(爬行者环→瞬移冲刺)
        case 55: creeperAI(this, game, p); break;                         // AI_055 爬行者(环绕漂移)
        case 30: twinsAI(this, game, p, false); break;                    // AI_030 雷神之眼 125(悬浮/连冲/变身/激光)
        case 31: twinsAI(this, game, p, true); break;                     // AI_031 魔神之眼 126(诅咒焰/火焰喷射)
        case 32: skeletronPrimeAI(this, game, p); break;                  // AI_032 机械骷髅王头 127(4 部件/旋冲/白天狂暴)
        case 33: case 34: case 35: case 36: primePartAI(this, game, p); break; // AI_033-036 Prime 部件(锯/激光/钳/炮)
        case 37: destroyerAI(this, game, p); break;                       // AI_037 毁灭者 134(80 段链穿地/探针激光)
        case 26: this.chargerAI(game, p); break;                        // AI_026 独角兽/冲锋族（原版核）
        case 107: this.chargerAI(game, p, 1); break;                    // AI_107 ImprovedWalkers（0.07/±1.0 walker 档，NPC.145.cs:43153 引擎核同构）
        default: this.zombieAI(game, p); break;                        // 其余家族待逐个移植
      }
    } else switch (this.key) {
      case 'slime_green':
      case 'slime_blue': this.slimeAI(game, hasPlayer ? player : null); break;
      case 'zombie': this.zombieAI(game, hasPlayer ? player : null); break;
      case 'demon_eye':
      case 'cave_bat': this.flyAI(game, hasPlayer ? player : null, 0.09); break;
      case 'servant_of_cthulhu': this.flyAI(game, hasPlayer ? player : null, 0.22); break;
      case 'eye_of_cthulhu': this.eocAI(game, player ?? null); break;
    }

    // 入水溅落声（与主角一致）：音量随与主角距离衰减，Boss 更响
    const eli = game.world.store.liquid[game.world.store.idx(
      Math.floor(this.cx / TILE), Math.floor(this.cy / TILE),
    )];
    const wasWater = this.inWater;
    this.inWater = eli > 80;
    if (this.inWater && !wasWater && hasPlayer) {
      const d = Math.hypot(player.cx - this.cx, player.cy - this.cy);
      const vol = Math.max(0.12, Math.min(1, 1 - d / (TILE * 45))) * (this.def.boss ? 1.2 : 0.9);
      game.playSfx('splash', vol);
    }

    // 接触伤害（带上攻击者引用，供荆棘反伤）。damage=0（小动物等友好生物）不触发——
    // 否则 damagePlayer(0) 仍会击退/闪红，表现为"兔子撞我有攻击性"
    if (hasPlayer && this.aabbOverlaps(player) && this.def.damage > 0) {
      game.damagePlayer(this.def.damage, this.cx, this.cy, this);
    }

    // Despawn 系统（原版 timeLeft/EncourageDespawn L7228-7241）：
    // 飘浮眼(2) 白天 DespawnEncouraged_AIStyle2（L19147）→ EncourageDespawn(10)；
    // 夜行怪白天离屏清除；其余离屏 timeLeft 递减到 0 清除（原版默认 7500 tick，回屏重置）
    const day = game.world.clock.isDay;
    if (hasPlayer) {
      const d = Math.hypot(player.cx - this.cx, player.cy - this.cy);
      if (!this.def.boss) {
        if ((day && this.def.nightOnly) || (day && this.vanilla?.aiStyle === 2)) {
          // 白天夜行怪/飘浮眼：离屏 >90 格即清
          if (d > TILE * 90) this.dead = true;
        } else if (d > TILE * 120) {
          this.despawnTimer--;
          if (this.despawnTimer <= 0) this.dead = true;
        } else {
          this.despawnTimer = 7500; // 原版默认 timeLeft，回屏重置
        }

19	  const map = new Map<number, string | null>();

31	      else if (ITEM_BY_KEY[`vi_${itemId}_${snake}`] != null) key = `vi_${itemId}_${snake}`;

33	    map.set(itemId, key);
34	    return key;
35	  };

52	  }
53	  _dropsCache.set(id, out);
54	  return out;

// 箭矢/通用直飞·抛物线投射物（原版 Projectile type 1/2、aiStyle 1/2 主干，Projectile.cs:546-566 + AI_001/AI_002）：
// 重力 0.3/tick（aiStyle1/2 通用常量；直飞弹传 0）、timeLeft 1200、旋转 atan2(vy,vx)+π/2（AI_001 尾部 L54877）、
// 原版贴图 Projectile_N.png；命中敌人伤害/击退/暴击（穿透>1 时同敌免疫防连击）；
// 命中 tileCut 砍草/碎罐（Projectile.CutTiles）；命中实心块 1/3 概率回收掉落。
import { Entity } from './Entity';
import { TILE } from '../core/constants';
import type { GameHooks } from './types';
import type { Renderer } from '../render/Renderer';
import type { Camera } from '../render/Camera';

/** 原版投射物贴图懒加载（Projectile_1.png = 木箭、Projectile_2.png = 燃烧箭，泛用所有 id） */
const spriteCache = new Map<number, HTMLImageElement>();
export function projSprite(projId: number): HTMLImageElement | null {
  let img = spriteCache.get(projId);
  if (img !== undefined) return img ?? null;
  if (typeof Image === 'undefined') return null;
  img = new Image();
  img.src = `sprites/vanilla/Projectile_${projId}.png`;
  spriteCache.set(projId, img);
  return img;
}

export interface ArrowOpts {
  /** 重力/tick（aiStyle1/2 = 0.3；直飞魔法弹传 0）。默认 0.3 */
  grav?: number;
  /** 原版 timeLeft（Projectile.cs:554 默认 1200） */
  life?: number;
  /** 穿透次数（原版 penetrate：手里剑 4、箭 1；-1 视作 1） */
  pierce?: number;
}

export class Arrow extends Entity {
  w = 10; h = 10; // 原版 SetDefaults type 1：width/height = 10
  vx: number;
  vy: number;
  damage: number;
  knockback: number;
  /** 原版投射物类型（1=木箭 2=燃烧箭，PickAmmo projToShoot = ammo.shoot） */
  projId: number;
  /** 回收掉落的 item key（null = 不回收，如燃烧箭） */
  dropKey: string | null;
  grav: number;
  life: number;
  pierce: number;
  /** 穿透投射物的同敌免疫表（敌人 id 集合） */
  private hitSet = new Set<number>();
  dead = false;

  constructor(x: number, y: number, vx: number, vy: number, damage: number,
    knockback: number, projId = 1, dropKey: string | null = null, opts?: ArrowOpts) {
    super();
    this.x = x; this.y = y;
    this.vx = vx; this.vy = vy;
    this.damage = damage;
    this.knockback = knockback;
    this.projId = projId;
    this.dropKey = dropKey;
    this.grav = opts?.grav ?? 0.3;
    this.life = opts?.life ?? 1200;
    this.pierce = opts?.pierce ?? 1;
  }

  draw(r: Renderer, _cam: Camera): void {
    // 世界坐标绘制(Renderer 实体循环运行在世界变换内,勿再自算屏幕坐标——
    // 曾双变换把箭甩出屏幕,表现为"箭隐形但能命中")
    const ctx = r.canvas.getContext('2d');
    if (!ctx) return;
    const ang = Math.atan2(this.vy, this.vx);
    const img = projSprite(this.projId);
    ctx.save();
    ctx.translate(this.x + this.w / 2, this.y + this.h / 2);
    ctx.rotate(ang + Math.PI / 2); // 原版贴图纵向：rotation = atan2+π/2（AI_001 L54877）
    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
    if (img && img.complete && img.naturalWidth > 0) {
      ctx.drawImage(img, -this.w / 2, -this.w / 2, this.w, this.w * (img.naturalHeight / img.naturalWidth));
    } else {
      // 贴图未就绪：短线兜底
      ctx.strokeStyle = this.projId === 2 ? '#FFB060' : '#D8C8A0';
      ctx.lineWidth = 2;
      ctx.beginPath();

Now design the three AIs.

=== AI_084 LunaticCultist (439), classic ===

Constants (classic):
- num = 120 (fireball interval, ai0=2 duration 4+120)
- attackDamage_ForProjectiles(35, 25) → classic 35 (ice mist 464 damage)
- num2 = 18, num3 = 3 (fireball: interval 18, count 3 → duration 4+18*3=58), dmg2 = GetAttackDamage(30,20) → 30

- state 1 (dash): on odd ticks undo move; ai1 -= 1 from num14*2... Note: vanilla enters state 1 with ai1 = num14*2 (set as nPC2.ai[1] = num14 * 2f). Then decrements. When ai1<=0 → state 0, ai3 += 1, velocity = 0.

Implementation: store dash velocity in e.vx/vy; state 1: if (Math.floor(ai1) % 2 !== 0 && ai1 !== 1) { x -= vx; y -= vy; } ai1 -= 1; integrate x += vx... wait vanilla order: `if ((int)ai1 % 2 != 0 && ai1 != 1) position -= velocity; ai1 -= 1;` and then... where does position += velocity happen? NPC position integration happens globally after AI() (NPC.VanillaUpdate → Collision). So per tick: global integration adds velocity; the AI subtracts it on odd ticks. Net: moves on even ticks. In our port, we integrate ourselves: move only when NOT (odd && !=1). I'll implement: `const step = !(Math.floor(e.ai1) % 2 !== 0 && e.ai1 !== 1); if (step) { e.x += e.vx; e.y += e.vy; } e.ai1 -= 1;`

```ts
function cultistPick(e: Enemy, phase2: boolean): number {
  const i = Math.floor(e.ai3);
  const table = phase2 ? [0,1,0,5,0,3,0,5,0,2,0,3,0,4] : [0,1,0,2,0,3,0,1,0,2,0,4];
  if (i < 0 || i >= table.length) { e.ai3 = -1; return 0; }
  if (i === table.length - 1) e.ai3 = -1;
  return table[i];
}

e.ai1 = 0; e.ai3 += 1; e.vx = 0; e.vy = 0; }\n    return;\n  }\n\n  // ---- ai0 == 5 仪式瞬移(:65623-65756):0-30 淡出,30 瞬移,30-90 隐形,90-120 淡入,\n  //      120-420 可被选中(打真身窗口);持续 420 ----\n  if (e.ai0 === 5) {\n    const t = e.ai1;\n    if (t >= 0 && t < 30) {\n      e.spawnAlpha = Math.floor((t / 30) * 254);\n      e.iframes = 2; k < 8; k++) {\n          const a = (k / 8) * Math.PI * 2;\n          const px = player.cx + Math.cos(a) * 300;\n          const py = player.cy + Math.sin(a) * 200;\n          const d = Math.hypot(px - e.cx, py - e.cy);\n          if (d > farD) { farD = d; n.y = ty * TILE + 8 - n.h / 2; n.vx = 0; n.vy = 0; n.bInit = true; }\n        break;\n      }\n    }\n    e.ai1 += 1;\n    if (e.ai1 >= 4 + 20 * 3) { e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai3 += 1; e.vx = 0; e.vy = 0; }\n    return;\n  }\n\n  // 未知状态:回选招\n  e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0;\n}\n\n// ===================== AI_120 光之女皇 636(NPC.cs:46299-47302) =====================\n// 状态机(ai0): 0 出生(180t) / 1 悬浮蓄力枢纽(DashTo 靠近 10t + 减速,45t(二阶段 20t)选招)/\n//   2 彩虹射线 873 / 4 光枪墙 919 / 5 永恒彩虹 872 / 6 太阳舞 923 / 7 光枪回环 919(二阶段)/\n//   8,9 刀阵冲刺(±50 速,6-40t 无敌蓄力,40-90t 伤害 ×1.5)/ 10 半血变身传送 /\n//   13 淡出退场(目标失效/超 6400px)。3(874 天降极光)与 11/12 为 expert 专属拍,跳过。\n// 经典弹幕伤害(:46326-46345): 919 墙 50 / 919 回环 70 / 873 45 / 872 45 / 923 50 / 874 40。\n// 选招表(:46400-46490): 一阶段 10 拍 [2,8,6,8,5,2,8,4,8,5] 循环,血 ≤50% 强制 10(变身);\n//   二阶段 9 拍 [7,2,8,5,2,6,6,4,8] 循环(expert 追加 11/12 拍已剔除)。\nconst EMPRESS_CYCLE_P1 = [2, 8, 6, 8, 5, 2, 8, 4, 8, 5];\nconst EMPRESS_CYCLE_P2 = [7, 2, 8, 5, 2, 6, 6, 4, 8];\n\n/** 原版 AI_120_HallowBoss_DashTo(:47304-47317):目标上移 300,>200 再回退 100,\n *  速度 = lerp(单位向量*min(len,18), vector/6, lerpValue(100,600,len)) */\nfunction empressDashTo(e: Enemy, tx0: number, ty0: number): void {\n  let tx = tx0, ty = ty0 - 300;\n  const d0 = Math.hypot(tx - e.cx, ty - e.cy);\n  if (d0 > 200) {\n    const ux = (tx - e.cx) / (d0 || 1), uy = (ty - e.cy) / (d0 || 1);\n    tx -= ux * 100; ty -= uy * 100;\n  }\n  const vx = tx - e.cx, vy = ty - e.cy;\n  const len = Math.hypot(vx, vy) || 1;\n  const lv = lerpValue(100, 600, len);\n  const cap = Math.min(len, 18);\n  e.vx = vLerp((vx / len) * cap, vx / 6, lv);\n  e.vy = vLerp((vy / len) * cap, vy / 6, lv);\n}\n\n/** 悬浮趋近(:46502 系):距 (玩家+偏移) > 40 才以 SimpleFlyMovement(dir*12, 0.5) 靠近 */\nfunction empressHover(e: Enemy, player: Player, ox: number, oy: number, spdMul = 1, accMul = 1): void {\n  const tx = player.cx + ox, ty = player.cy + oy;\n  if (Math.hypot(tx - e.cx, ty - e.cy) > 40) {\n    const dx = tx - e.cx, dy = ty - e.cy;\n    const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;\n    simpleFly(e, (dx / d) * 12 * spdMul, (dy / d) * 12 * spdMul, 0.5 * accMul);\n  }\n}\n\nexport function empressOfLightAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null): void {\n  if (!e.bInit) {\n    e.bInit = true;\n    e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; e.ai3 = 0;\n    e.spawnAlpha = 254; // Opacity = ai1/180 渐显(:46410)\n    game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.cy - 80, '#FFE080', 20, 2.5);\n  }\n  const phase2 = e.ai3 === 1 || e.ai3 === 3; // AI_120_HallowBoss_IsInPhase2 :46179\n  const b = baseOf(e);\n  // :47296-47301 二阶段防御 ×1.2(ShouldEmpressBeEnraged 白天暴怒分支按任务不实现:白天不逃不暴怒)\n  e.def.defense = Math.floor(phase2 ? b.def * 1.2 : b.def);\n\n  // 目标失效/超 6400px → 淡出退场(:46488-46491 num36=13)\n  if ((!player || player.dead || Math.hypot(player.cx - e.cx, player.cy - e.cy) > 6400) && e.ai0 !== 13) {\n    e.ai0 = 13; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 += 1;\n    e.vx /= 4; e.vy /= 4;\n  }\n  if (!player) { flyIntegrate(e); return; }\n  e.facing = player.cx > e.cx ? 1 : -1;\n\n  // 冲刺窗口伤害倍率(num16,:46352/:47006):8/9 态 40-90t 时 ×1.5\n  const dashBoost = (e.ai0 === 8 || e.ai0 === 9) && e.ai1 > 40 && e.ai1 <= 90;\n  e.def.damage = Math.round(b.dmg * (dashBoost ? 1.5 : 1));\n\n  switch (e.ai0) {\n    // ---- 0 出生(:46377-46417):velocity=(0,5) 后 0.95 阻尼,180t 渐显 ----\n    case 0: {\n      if (e.ai1 === 0) { e.vx = 0; e.vy = 5; }\n      e.vx *= 0.95; e.vy *= 0.95;\n      e.spawnAlpha = Math.max(0, 254 - Math.floor(clamp(e.ai1 / 180, 0, 1) * 254));\n      e.ai1 += 1;\n      flyIntegrate(e);\n      if (e.ai1 >= 180) { e.ai0 = 1; e.ai1 = 0; }\n      break;\n    }\n    // ---- 1 悬浮蓄力枢纽(:46418-46511):前 10t DashTo 冲近,之后阻尼;到时按表选招 ----\n    case 1: {\n      const dur = phase2 ? 20 : 45; // num34 :46419(expert/getGoodWorld 折半分支不实现)\n      if (e.ai1 <= 10) empressDashTo(e, player.cx, player.cy);\n      if (Math.hypot(e.vx, e.vy) > 16 && e.ai1 > 10) { e.vx /= 2; e.vy /= 2; }\n      e.vx *= 0.92; e.vy *= 0.92;\n      e.ai1 += 1;\n      if (e.ai1 < dur) { flyIntegrate(e); break; }\n      const table = phase2 ? EMPRESS_CYCLE_P2 : EMPRESS_CYCLE_P1;\n      let next = table[Math.floor(e.ai2) % table.length];\n      if (!phase2 && e.hp / e.maxHp <= 0.5) next = 10; // :46463 半血强制变身\n      if (next === 8 && player.cx > e.cx) next = 9;     // :46493 冲刺方向\n      e.ai0 = next; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 += 1;\n      break;\n    }\n    // ---- 2 彩虹射线 873(:46512-46649):悬玩家左上(-150,-250),每 3t 一发直飞,60t 止 ----\n    case 2: {\n      empressHover(e, player, -150, -250);\n      const dur = 60 + (90 - (phase2 ? 15 : 0)); // num90 - num17 :46517/:46356\n      if (Math.floor(e.ai1) % 3 === 0 && e.ai1 < 60) {\n        const [dvx, dvy] = rot(0, -6, (Math.PI / 2) * (Math.random() * 2 - 1));\n        shoot(game, e.cx - 55, e.cy - 30, dvx, dvy, 45, 873);\n      }\n      e.ai1 += 1;\n      if (e.ai1 >= dur) { e.ai0 = 1; e.ai1 = 0; }\n      break;\n    }\n    // ---- 4 光枪墙 919(:46695-46779):悬玩家上 (0,-350),每 4t 在玩家周围 300px 环上布枪 ----\n    case 4: {\n      empressHover(e, player, 0, -350);\n      const dur = 100 + (20 - (phase2 ? 15 : 0));\n      if (Math.floor(e.ai1) % 4 === 0 && e.ai1 < 100) {\n        const num82 = 4; // 经典枪数(expert 5)\n        const num85 = Math.floor(e.ai1) / 4;\n        let ux = Math.cos(Math.PI / (num82 * 2) + num85 * (Math.PI / num82));\n        let uy = Math.sin(Math.PI / (num82 * 2) + num85 * (Math.PI / num82));\n        ux += ux > 0 ? 0.5 : -0.5; // 经典横向外扩(:46715-46718)\n        const ul = Math.hypot(ux, uy) || 1; ux /= ul; uy /= ul;\n        if (Math.hypot(player.cx - e.cx, player.cy - e.cy) > 2400) { e.ai1 += 1; flyIntegrate(e); break; }\n        if (player.vx * ux + player.vy * uy > 0) { ux = -ux; uy = -uy; } // 逆着玩家速度布枪\n        const leadX = player.cx + player.vx * 90, leadY = player.cy + player.vy * 90;\n        let px = player.cx + ux * 300 - player.vx * 30;\n        let py = player.cy + uy * 300 - player.vy * 30;\n        const pd = Math.hypot(px - player.cx, py - player.cy);\n        if (pd < 300) { // 环距不足外推(:46724-46731)\n          const ex = player.cx - px, ey = player.cy - py;\n          const el = Math.hypot(ex, ey) || 1;\n          px = player.cx - (ex / el) * 300; py = player.cy - (ey / el) * 300;\n        }\n        const ax = leadX - px, ay = leadY - py;\n        const al = Math.hypot(ax, ay) || 1;\n        // 原版 919 零速预告后沿 v4 方向冲刺(Projectile.AI 收敛:12 速直飞)\n        shoot(game, px, py, (ax / al) * 12, (ay / al) * 12, 50, 919, 240);\n      }\n      e.ai1 += 1;\n      if (e.ai1 >= dur) { e.ai0 = 1; e.ai1 = 0; }\n      break;\n    }\n    // ---- 5 永恒彩虹 872(:46780-46870):悬玩家上,开场 13 发环形彩弹(8 速) ----\n    case 5: {\n      empressHover(e, player, 0, -350);\n      const dur = 42 + (30 - (phase2 ? 15 : 0));\n      if (Math.floor(e.ai1) % 42 === 0 && e.ai1 < 42) {\n        const base = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;\n        for (let k = 0; k < 13; k++) {\n          const t = k / 13;\n          const [ux, uy] = rot(0, 1, Math.PI / 2 + Math.PI * 2 * t + base);\n          const [ox, oy] = rot(ux, uy, -Math.PI / 2);\n          shoot(game, e.cx + 55 + ox * 30, e.cy - 30 + oy * 30, ux * 8, uy * 8, 45, 872);\n        }\n        game.playSfx('roar', 0.4);\n      }\n      e.ai1 += 1;\n      if (e.ai1 >= dur) { e.ai0 = 1; e.ai1 = 0; }\n      break;\n    }\n    // ---- 6 太阳舞 923(:46871-46935):悬玩家上 (-80,-500) 低速,每 60t 一环 6 发,3 环 ----\n    case 6: {\n      empressHover(e, player, -80, -500, 0.3, 0.7);\n      const dur = 180 + (120 - (phase2 ? 15 : 0));\n      if (Math.floor(e.ai1) % 60 === 0 && e.ai1 < 180) {\n        const volley = Math.floor(e.ai1) / 60;\n        const flip = player.cx > e.cx ? 1 : 0;\n        for (let t = 0; t < 1; t += 1 / 6) {\n          const slot = (t + (1 / 6) * 0.5 + volley * (1 / 6) * 0.5) % 1;\n          const a = Math.PI * 2 * (slot + flip);\n          // 原版 923 零速绕体公转(收敛:8 速放射)\n          shoot(game, e.cx, e.cy - 100, Math.cos(a) * 8, Math.sin(a) * 8, 50, 923, 240);\n        }\n      }\n      e.ai1 += 1;\n      if (e.ai1 >= dur) { e.ai0 = 1; e.ai1 = 0; }\n      break;\n    }\n    // ---- 7 光枪回环 919(二阶段,:46936-47049):每 60t 以玩家为中心布 13 连枪线,4 线 ----\n    case 7: {\n      empressHover(e, player, 0, -350, 0.4, 1);\n      const dur = 240 + (20 - (phase2 ? 15 : 0));\n      if (Math.floor(e.ai1) % 60 === 0 && e.ai1 < 240) {\n        const line = Math.floor(e.ai1) / 60;\n        let cx0 = player.cx, cy0 = player.cy;\n        let dirX = 0, dirY = 0, axX = 0, axY = 0;\n        const span = 1950; // num76 = 13*150 :46961\n        switch (line) { // :46976-47011 四线几何\n          case 0: cx0 += -span / 2; dirX = 0; dirY = span; axX = 1; axY = 0; break;\n          case 1: cx0 += span / 2; cy0 += 75; dirX = 0; dirY = span; axX = -1; axY = 0; break;\n          case 2: cx0 += -span * 0.4; cy0 += -span * 0.4; dirX = span * 1.4; dirY = 0; axX = 1; axY = 1; break;\n          default: cx0 += span * 0.4 + 75; cy0 += -span * 0.4; dirX = -span * 1.4; dirY = 0; axX = -1; axY = 1; break;\n        }\n        const al = Math.hypot(axX, axY) || 1;\n        for (let t = 0; t <= 1.0001; t += 1 / 13) {\n          const ox = cx0 + dirX * (t - 0.5), oy = cy0 + dirY * (t - 0.5);\n          shoot(game, ox, oy, (axX / al) * 12, (axY / al) * 12, 70, 919, 240);\n        }\n        game.playSfx('roar', 0.45);\n      }\n      e.ai1 += 1;\n      if (e.ai1 >= dur) { e.ai0 = 1; e.ai1 = 0; }\n      break;\n    }\n    // ---- 8/9 刀阵冲刺(:47053-47089):0-40t 冲到玩家侧后 550px(6-40t 无敌),\n    //      40-90t 以 ±50 横速突进(伤害 ×1.5),其后 0.92 阻尼 ----\n    case 8:\n    case 9: {\n      const side = e.ai0 === 8 ? -1 : 1; // num33 :47056\n      if (e.ai1 <= 40) {\n        if (e.ai1 >= 6) e.iframes = 2; // flag6 → dontTakeDamage(:47058)\n        const destX = player.cx + side * -550, destY = player.cy;\n        const dx = destX - e.cx, dy = destY - e.cy;\n        const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;\n        simpleFly(e, (dx / d) * 12, (dy / d) * 12, 1.0);\n        if (e.ai1 === 40) { e.vx *= 0.3; e.vy *= 0.3; }\n      } else if (e.ai1 <= 90) {\n        e.vx = vLerp(side * 50, e.vx, 0.05);\n        e.vy = vLerp(0, e.vy, 0.05);\n        if (e.ai1 === 90) { e.vx *= 0.7; e.vy *= 0.7; }\n      } else {\n        e.vx *= 0.92; e.vy *= 0.92;\n      }\n      e.ai1 += 1;\n      if (e.ai1 >= 90 + (20 - (phase2 ? 15 : 0))) { e.ai0 = 1; e.ai1 = 0; }\n      break;\n    }\n    // ---- 10 半血变身(:47106-47135):90t 时传送玩家上 250 处并 ai3 0→1(进入二阶段) ----\n    case 10: {\n      if (e.ai1 >= 30 && e.ai1 <= 170) e.iframes = 2; // flag6 → dontTakeDamage(:47110)\n      e.vx *= 0.95; e.vy *= 0.95;\n      if (e.ai1 === 90) {\n        if (e.ai3 === 0) e.ai3 = 1; // ai3==2(白天暴怒)分支不实现\n        e.x = player.cx - e.w / 2;\n        e.y = player.cy - 250 - e.h / 2;\n        game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.cy, '#FFEE90', 24, 3);\n        game.playSfx('roar', 0.8);\n      }\n      e.ai1 += 1;\n      if (e.ai1 >= 180 + (20 - (phase2 ? 15 : 0))) { e.ai0 = 1; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; }\n      break;\n    }\n    // ---- 13 淡出退场(:47226-47281):alpha 渐隐,全隐后移除 ----\n    case 13: {\n      e.spawnAlpha = Math.min(254, e.spawnAlpha + 5);\n      e.ai1 += 1;\n      flyIntegrate(e);\n      if (e.ai1 >= 20 && e.spawnAlpha >= 254) e.dead = true;\n      break;\n    }\n    default:\n      e.ai0 = 1; e.ai1 = 0;\n      break;\n  }\n  if (e.ai0 !== 13) e.spawnAlpha = Math.max(0, e.spawnAlpha - 5); // :47292 alpha 回落\n  flyIntegrate(e);\n}\n\n// ===================== AI_121 史莱姆皇后 657(NPC.cs:45543-46177) =====================\n// 状态机(ai0): 0 待机(一阶段地面/二阶段 FlyMovement) / 1 传送后显形 / 2 传送消散 /\n//   3 走路连跳(-8,-8,-6,-13 四连) / 4 升空砸地(ai2=0 升空 60t → ai2=1 重力坠,落地撒 922) /\n//   5 凝胶环(ai2=0 蓄 50t → ai2=1 撒 926 环)。\n// 半血(flag2)进二阶段:悬浮 FlyMovement + 攻速变化 + 暴怒(离开神圣之地)分支按任务跳过。\n// 经典数值(:45546-45552): 砸地 922 40 伤 / 凝胶环 926 30 伤;砸地蓄力 30t(二阶段 10t);\n//   凝胶环 6 发(二阶段 10 发);仆从按掉血 2%(二阶段 1.5%)撒 1-2 只 NPC 658/659/660。\nconst QUEEN_MINION_VANILLA = [658, 659, 660]; // vanilla :46168-46173\nconst QUEEN_MINION_FALLBACK = [535, 536, 537]; // 658-660 未入 vanilla-npcs.json → 用独立水晶/弹跳/飞翔史莱姆\n\n/** AI_121_QueenSlime_FlyMovement(:45467-45542):12 速/0.085 加速度趋近玩家上方 250,\n *  反向且 >100px 时加速度 ×2;旋转 = vx*0.1 钳 ±0.5 */\nfunction queenFlyMovement(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player): void {\n  e.onGround = false;\n  const spd = 12, accBase = 0.085, hover = 250;\n  let tx: number, ty: number;\n  if (losClear(game, e.cx, e.cy, player.cx, player.cy)) {\n    tx = player.cx; ty = player.cy - hover;\n  } else {\n    // :45478-45496 无视线 → 逐级上探玩家上方第一个实心格下方(简化:玩家上方 16px 一档,探 16 档)\n    const st = game.world.store;\n    let found = false;\n    for (let i = 0; i < 16; i++) {\n      const py = player.cy - 16 * i;\n      const ptx = Math.floor(player.cx / TILE), pty = Math.floor(py / TILE);\n      if (st.inBounds(ptx, pty) && st.isSolid(ptx, pty)) {\n        tx = player.cx; ty = py + 16;\n        found = true;\n        break;\n      }\n    }\n    if (!found) { tx = player.cx; ty = player.cy; }\n  }\n  let dvx = tx - e.cx, dvy = ty - e.cy;\n  const dist = Math.hypot(dvx, dvy);\n  let acc = accBase;\n  if (Math.abs(dvx) < 40) dvx = e.vx;                       // :45512-45514\n  const lim = spd - 2;\n  if (dist > 100 && ((e.vx < -lim && dvx > 0) || (e.vx > lim && dvx < 0))) acc *= 2; // :45516-45519\n  if (dist < 40) { dvx = e.vx; dvy = e.vy; }                // :45521-45522\n  else if (dist < 80) { dvx = (dvx / (dist || 1)) * spd * 0.65; dvy = (dvy / (dist || 1)) * spd * 0.65; }\n  else { dvx = (dvx / (dist || 1)) * spd; dvy = (dvy / (dist || 1)) * spd; }\n  simpleFly(e, dvx, dvy, acc);\n  e.spin = clamp(e.vx * 0.1, -0.5, 0.5); // :45536-45541\n}\n\nexport function queenSlimeAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null): void {\n  const la = laOf(e);\n  if (!e.bInit) {\n    // :45559-45566 localAI[0]==0:ai[1]=-100,localAI[0]=lifeMax\n    e.bInit = true;\n    e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = -100; e.ai2 = 0; e.ai3 = 0;\n    la.la0 = e.maxHp;\n  }\n  const phase2 = e.hp <= e.maxHp / 2; // flag2 :45552\n  // 掉血阈值撒仆从(:46147-46177):localAI[0] 记上次标记血量,差值超 2%(二阶段 1.5%)即撒\n  if (la.la0 >= e.maxHp / 2 && e.hp < e.maxHp / 2) {\n    la.la0 = e.hp;            // :46142-46148 进二阶段复位状态\n    e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0;\n  }\n  const b = baseOf(e);\n  e.def.damage = b.dmg;\n  e.def.defense = b.def;\n  // 玩家死/超 500 格 → 鼓励离场(EncourageDespawn:45576;简化为远距上浮,不清除)\n  if (!player || player.dead || Math.abs(e.cx - player.cx) / 16 > 500) {\n    e.vy -= 0.06;\n    e.y += e.vy; e.x += e.vx;\n    e.spin = clamp(e.vx * 0.1, -0.5, 0.5);\n    return;\n  }\n  const dirToPlayer = player.cx >= e.cx ? 1 : -1;\n  e.facing = dirToPlayer;\n\n  // 传送蓄能(ai3,一阶段专属:45582-45600):无视线或高差 >320 时 +1.5/t,否则 -1/t;\n  // 满 300 且在地面待机 → 进传送(2)。KingSlime_FindTeleportSpot 收敛:玩家附近随机落点。\n  if (!phase2) {\n    const blocked = !losClear(game, e.cx, e.cy, player.cx, player.cy) || Math.abs(e.y - (player.y + player.h)) > 320;\n    if (blocked) e.ai3 += 1.5;\n    else e.ai3 = Math.max(0, e.ai3 - 1);\n    if (e.ai3 >= 300 && e.ai0 === 0 && e.vy === 0 && e.onGround) {\n      const st = game.world.store;\n      const ptx = Math.floor(player.cx / TILE);\n      let ok = false;\n      for (let tries = 0; tries < 60 && !ok; tries++) {\n        const tx = ptx + (Math.random() < 0.5 ? -1 : 1) * (30 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 30));\n        if (!st.inBounds(tx, 0)) continue;\n        for (let ty = Math.max(1, Math.floor(player.cy / TILE) - 40); ty < st.h - 2; ty++) {\n          if (st.isSolid(tx, ty) && !st.isSolid(tx, ty - 1) && !st.isSolid(tx, ty - 2)) {\n            la.la1 = tx * TILE + 8;\n            la.la2 = ty * TILE;\n            ok = true;\n            break;\n          }\n        }\n      }\n      if (ok) { e.ai0 = 2; e.ai1 = 0; }\n      else e.ai3 = 0;\n    }\n  }\n\n  const flying = phase2 && (e.ai0 === 0 || e.ai0 === 5 || (e.ai0 === 4 && e.ai2 === 0));\n  if (!flying) {\n    // 地面态重力(原版全局 gravity;与 bossAI.kingSlimeAI 同取 0.35)\n    if (!e.onGround && e.ai0 !== 4) e.vy += 0.35;\n    if (e.vy > 16) e.vy = 16;\n  } else {\n    e.onGround = false;\n  }\n\n  switch (e.ai0) {\n    // ---- 0 待机(:45628-45686):地面摩擦 / 二阶段飞行;ai1 过阈值(60/120)选下一招 ----\n    case 0: {\n      if (phase2) {\n        queenFlyMovement(e, game, player);\n      } else if (e.onGround) {\n        e.vx *= 0.8;\n        if (Math.abs(e.vx) < 0.1) e.vx = 0;\n        e.spin = 0;\n      }\n      e.ai1 += 1;\n      const idle = phase2 ? 120 : 60; // num10 :45648-45652\n      if (e.ai1 > idle) {\n        e.ai1 = 0;\n        if (phase2) {\n          // :45660-45678 二阶段:默认砸地(ai2=1),玩家在上方或横距 >250 改凝胶环\n          let next = Math.random() < 0.5 ? 4 : 5;\n          let sub = next === 4 ? 1 : 0;\n          if (next === 4 && (player.y + player.h < e.y + e.h || Math.abs(player.cx - e.cx) > 250)) {\n            next = 5; sub = 0;\n          }\n          e.ai0 = next; e.ai2 = sub;\n        } else {\n          // :45680-45686 一阶段:随机 跳(3)/砸地(4)/凝胶环(5)\n          const r = Math.floor(Math.random() * 3);\n          e.ai0 = r === 0 ? 3 : r === 1 ? 4 : 5;\n          e.ai2 = 0;\n        }\n      }\n      break;\n    }\n    // ---- 1 传送后显形(:45689-45720):30t;缩放动画(num3)为纯视觉,跳过 ----\n    case 1: {\n      e.spin = 0;\n      game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.cy, '#7040A0', 2, 1.2);\n      e.ai1 += 1;\n      if (e.ai1 >= 30) { e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; }\n      break;\n    }\n    // ---- 2 传送消散(:45721-45758):60t 后落位到 la1/la2(底部对齐) ----\n    case 2: {\n      e.spin = 0;\n      e.ai1 += 1;\n      if (e.ai1 >= 60 && la.la1 !== 0) {\n        e.iframes = 2; // dontTakeDamage = hide(:46138)\n        e.x = la.la1 - e.w / 2;\n        e.y = la.la2 - e.h;\n        e.vx = 0; e.vy = 0;\n        e.ai0 = 1; e.ai1 = 0;\n        game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.cy, '#7040A0', 16, 2.4);\n      } else if (e.ai1 > 90) {\n        e.ai0 = 1; e.ai1 = 0; // 找不到落点的兜底\n      }\n      break;\n    }\n    // ---- 3 走路连跳(:45759-45844):四连跳 vy=-8/-8/-6/-13,横速递增 4/4.5/4/3.5;\n    //      落地重跳倒计时 ai1(血量 <66%/<33% 各 +4/t 加速) ----\n    case 3: {\n      e.spin = 0;\n      if (e.onGround) {\n        e.vx *= 0.8;\n        if (Math.abs(e.vx) < 0.1) e.vx = 0;\n        e.ai1 += 4;\n        if (e.hp < e.maxHp * 0.66) e.ai1 += 4;\n        if (e.hp < e.maxHp * 0.33) e.ai1 += 4;\n        if (e.ai1 >= 0) {\n          if (e.ai2 === 3) {\n            e.vy = -13; e.vx += 3.5 * dirToPlayer;\n            e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0;\n            e.ai0 = 0; // 大跳结束回待机\n            game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.y + e.h, '#8060C0', 10, 2.2);\n          } else if (e.ai2 === 2) {\n            e.vy = -6; e.vx += 4.5 * dirToPlayer;\n            e.ai1 = -40; e.ai2 += 1;\n          } else {\n            e.vy = -8; e.vx += 4 * dirToPlayer;\n            e.ai1 = -40; e.ai2 += 1;\n          }\n          e.onGround = false;\n        }\n      } else {\n        // 空中横移(:45819-45844):朝玩家 0.2/t,上限 3(反向时 0.93 阻尼)\n        const cap = 3;\n        if ((dirToPlayer === 1 && e.vx < cap) || (dirToPlayer === -1 && e.vx > -cap)) {\n          if ((dirToPlayer === -1 && e.vx < 0.1) || (dirToPlayer === 1 && e.vx > -0.1)) {\n            e.vx += 0.2 * dirToPlayer;\n          } else {\n            e.vx *= 0.93;\n          }\n        }\n      }\n      break;\n    }\n    // ---- 4 升空砸地(:45845-46006):ai2=0 飞向玩家上 384(60t) → ai2=1 重力坠(蓄力 30/10t,\n    //      vy+1/t 钳 14);落地撒 922 冲击波(40 伤)+ 尘暴,回待机 ----\n    case 4: {\n      e.spin *= 0.9;\n      if (e.ai2 === 1) {\n        const num13 = phase2 ? 10 : 30; // num17 = 14(经典;getGoodWorld 15.99 不实现)\n          } else {\n            e.vy *= 0.8;\n          }\n          if (phase2 && e.ai1 > num13 + 120) { // 二阶段超时兜底(:45931-45938)\n            e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; e.vy *= 0.8;\n          }\n        } else {\n          // 落地冲击(:45870-45890):922 冲击波 + 尘暴 + 回待机\n          shoot(game, e.cx, e.y + e.h, 0, 0, 40, 922, 240);\n          game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.y + e.h, '#B090E0', 20, 3);\n          game.playSfx('roar', 0.7);\n          e.ai0 = 0; e.ai2 = 1;\n          e.vy = -3; // :45974-45979\n          e.vx *= 0.5;\n        }\n      }\n      break;\n    }\n    // ---- 5 凝胶环(:46007-46121):ai2=0 蓄 50t(二阶段兼飞)→ ai2=1 第 10t 撒 926 环\n    //      (6 发(二阶段 10 发),30 伤,9 速,-i*2π/N) ----\n    case 5: {\n      e.spin *= 0.9;\n      if (e.ai2 === 1) {\n        e.ai1 += 1;\n        if (e.ai1 >= 10) {\n          const count = phase2 ? 10 : 6; // num6/num7 :46024-46031\n          for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {\n            const a = -i * (Math.PI * 2) / 10;\n            shoot(game, e.cx, e.cy, Math.cos(a) * 9, Math.sin(a) * 9, 30, 926);\n          }\n          game.playSfx('roar', 0.5);\n          e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0;\n      break;\n  }\n\n  // 积分 + 碰撞(飞行态无碰撞)\n  e.x += e.vx;\n  e.y += e.vy;\n  if (!flying) collideGround(e, game);\n\n  if (e.hp <= 0) return;\n\n  // 掉血撒仆从(:46147-46177):差值超阈值 → 1-2 只,本体附近随机落点,ai0=-500*rand3\n  const threshold = Math.floor(e.maxHp * (phase2 ? 0.015 : 0.02));\n  if (e.hp + threshold < la.la0) {\n    la.la0 = e.hp;\n    const n = 1 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 2);
